### [173] [Sparse Principal Component Analysis via Wavelets for Distributed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05386)
*Giovanni Barbosa Herrero,Rodney Vasconcelos Fonseca,Aluísio Pinheiro*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出将Johnstone & Lu (2009)的小波稀疏化方法与Fan et al. (2019)的分布式PCA框架相结合，以在降低通信成本的同时保持特征空间估计质量。模拟实验表明，在维度超过一定阈值后，该方法在估计误差上优于Fan et al. (2019)，且全程传输更少的系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习场景下，数据分布在多台机器上，无法直接访问全样本。Johnstone & Lu (2009)指出PCA在高维情形下统计不一致，并通过小波稀疏化恢复一致性；Fan et al. (2019)实现了分布式环境下的特征空间估计，但未处理高维问题。因此，需要一种既能应对高维挑战、又能降低通信成本的方法。

Method: 将Johnstone & Lu (2009)提出的基于小波的稀疏化（wavelet-based sparsification）和变量选择技术，嵌入到Fan et al. (2019)的分布式PCA框架中。通过小波变换对数据进行稀疏表示，仅传输稀疏化后的系数，从而降低节点间通信开销，同时在聚合端恢复对特征空间的估计。

Result: 在维度d ∈ [52, 5000]范围内的模拟实验中，所提方法在维度超过明确阈值（λ=25时d≥152，λ=50时d≥252）后，估计误差低于Fan et al. (2019)方法，且在整个研究维度范围内系统性地传输了更少的系数。

Conclusion: 将小波稀疏化与分布式PCA相结合，可在不牺牲估计质量的前提下有效降低通信成本。该方法在高维场景下尤其具有优势，当维度超过一定阈值后，估计精度显著优于现有分布式PCA方法。

Abstract: The large volume of data and concerns about data privacy have motivated the development of techniques for distributed data, a problem also known as federated learning. In this scenario, sub-samples of the data are divided across different machines, and statistics must be computed over that data without direct access to the full sample. Johnstone & Lu (2009, JASA) show that principal component analysis (PCA) is statistically inconsistent in the high-dimensional regime, and propose a way to recover consistency through wavelet-based sparsification and variable selection. Fan et al. (2019, AoS) show a way to perform this same estimation -- specifically, to estimate the eigenspace that would be obtained if all the data were pooled together, even though it remains effectively distributed -- without addressing the high-dimensional regime. This work incorporates the wavelet-based sparsification of Johnstone & Lu (2009) into the distributed PCA framework of Fan et al. (2019), aiming to reduce communication cost without compromising the quality of the eigenspace estimation. Simulations across $d \in [52, 5000]$ show that the proposed method overtakes Fan et al. (2019) in estimation error beyond a clear dimensional threshold ($d \geq 152$ for $λ=25$, $d \geq 252$ for $λ=50$), while transmitting systematically fewer coefficients throughout the entire range studied. This study was financed by the Sao Paulo Research Foundation (FAPESP), Brazil. Process Number #2023/02538-0 and Number #2025/21250-2.

</details>


### [174] [Inference for subgraph densities in noisy dynamic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05407)
*Peter W. MacDonald,Eric D. Kolaczyk*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对动态网络序列中子图密度估计与推断的统计方法，能够显式修正网络边的观测误差，并基于隐马尔可夫网络模型实现对非相同网络副本的鲁棒推断及多快照的高效聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chang et al. (2022) 指出了噪声网络分析中的两个关键开放问题：如何应对非同分布的网络副本，以及如何高效聚合多个网络快照。本文旨在解决这两个问题，将方法扩展至动态观测的网络数据场景。

Method: 通过设定一个随机演化的隐马尔可夫网络模型，开发了针对时间索引动态网络序列的子图密度估计与推断方法，显式建模并修正网络边的观测误差，并将方法扩展至多时间点子图密度的联合推断。

Result: 所提方法在理论上具有良好的渐近性质（随网络规模增长），并能处理非相同网络副本和多个网络快照的高效聚合，同时还支持对不同时间点网络快照进行正式的统计比较。

Conclusion: 该方法大幅拓展了噪声网络分析的数据适用范围，使得对动态网络快照的统计比较成为可能，为动态网络序列的子图密度推断提供了统一且严谨的统计框架。

Abstract: In this work we develop statistical methodology to estimate and perform inference on subgraph densities using time-indexed, or dynamic network sequences. These estimates explicitly adjust for observation errors for the network edges, and have good theoretical properties as the size of the network grows. By specifying a stochastically evolving hidden Markov network model, we address two important directions for further investigation identified by Chang et al. (2022): robustness to non-identical network replicates, and efficient aggregation of multiple available network snapshots. These new methods vastly expand the analysis of noisy networks to new data settings, as network replicates are commonly observed dynamically. The methodology is also extended to consider joint inference for subgraph densities at multiple time points, to facilitate formal statistical comparison of dynamic network snapshots.

</details>


### [175] [Risk-Aware Quantile Learning for Personalized Dynamic Treatment Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05434)
*Chunyin Lei,Annie Qu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出风险感知的分位数动态治疗方案框架(RQDTR)，在优化累积结局预设分位数的同时显式纳入治疗相关风险，并支持多类别治疗决策，实现尾部疗效与获益-风险权衡的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分位数动态治疗方案(DTR)方法存在两大局限：一是仅关注疗效目标，忽略了治疗相关风险的控制；二是主要局限于二元治疗场景，难以处理多类别治疗选择。临床实践中，医生需要同时优化结局分布的尾部特征、控制治疗风险，并在多种治疗方案中做出选择。

Method: 提出RQDTR统一框架：（1）在优化累积潜在结局预设分位数时显式纳入治疗风险；（2）开发基于角度的公式化方法实现多类别治疗决策规则的联合学习；（3）构建三个可解释子类：仅疗效分位数学习、基于群体水平风险控制的约束学习、以及基于复合获益-风险效用的效用学习。理论方面建立了识别性与神谕等价性、平滑替代的Fisher一致性、估计方案的相合性及有限样本误差率。

Result: 大量模拟研究表明RQDTR在尾部疗效上优于现有方法，并实现更优的获益-风险权衡。在All of Us抑郁症数据和MIMIC-III脓毒症数据的实际应用中，RQDTR同样展现出相对于现有分位数DTR方法的显著改进。

Conclusion: RQDTR成功将风险感知机制与分位数优化统一于动态治疗决策框架中，兼具多类别治疗支持与灵活的目标设定（仅疗效、约束型、效用型），为个性化序贯临床决策提供了更全面且实用的方法论。

Abstract: Sequential clinical decision-making often involves more than maximizing average efficacy. Clinicians may need to simultaneously optimize clinically relevant tails of the outcome distribution, control treatment-related risk, and choose among multiple treatment options. Existing quantile dynamic treatment regime (DTR) methods capture distributional features of treatment outcomes but remain largely restricted to efficacy-only objectives and binary treatments. To address these limitations, we propose Risk-Aware Quantile Dynamic Treatment Regimes (RQDTR), a unified framework that optimizes a prespecified quantile of the cumulative potential outcome while explicitly incorporating treatment-related risk. We also develop an angle-based formulation for jointly learning decision rules across multiple treatment categories. Our framework includes three interpretable subclasses: efficacy-only quantile learning, constraint-based learning with population-level risk control, and utility-based learning through a composite benefit-risk utility. Theoretically, we establish identification and oracle equivalence, Fisher consistency of the smoothed surrogate, consistency of the estimated regime, and finite-sample performance error rates. Extensive simulation studies and applications to All of Us major depressive disorder and MIMIC-III sepsis data demonstrate that RQDTR improves tail-oriented efficacy and achieves more favorable benefit-risk trade-offs than existing quantile DTR methods.

</details>


### [176] [Fast Power Evaluation under Biased-Coin Minimization: Sampling and Randomization Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05527)
*Masahiro Kojima*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出可重用的层不平衡高斯近似（SIGA）框架，通过将固定得分统计量分解为联合层不平衡和正交层内分量，大幅降低有偏硬币最小化下设计阶段功效和样本量评估的计算负担。其中 SIGA-S 针对均值或风险差边界估计重复抽样方差，SIGA-R 则通过配对分配校准近似条件参考分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在设计阶段基于有偏硬币最小化进行功效与样本量评估时，需在每个模拟试验中重复执行预设随机化检验，导致计算极为密集，亟需一种高效且准确的近似方法以降低计算成本。

Method: 作者提出可重用的层不平衡高斯近似（SIGA）框架：首先将固定得分统计量精确分解为联合层不平衡分量与正交的层内分量；在相同绝对不平衡规则的显式分配-复制极限条件下，SIGA-S 程序在边际均值或风险差边界处一致估计重复抽样方差；针对非锐利边界下条件方差与无条件方差的差异，以配对路径协方差的二次型刻画一阶方差间隙，并引入基于可重用配对分配校准的 SIGA-R 程序来近似条件参考分布。

Result: 综合性基准测试表明，SIGA-S 和 SIGA-R 各自与对应的参考随机化检验高度吻合。基于已发表汇总规划特征的试验模拟同样显示，SIGA-S、SIGA-R 与参考随机化检验的功效相近，且两种可重用校准程序均显著减少了相对于嵌套重随机化的计算量。

Conclusion: SIGA 框架能够在保持与参考随机化检验高度一致的前提下，大幅降低有偏硬币最小化设计中功效与样本量评估的计算强度，具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Design-stage power and sample-size evaluation under biased-coin minimization can be computationally intensive when a prespecified randomization test is reproduced within every simulated trial. We develop a reusable stratum-imbalance Gaussian approximation (SIGA) framework by exactly decomposing a fixed-score statistic into joint-stratum imbalance and orthogonal within-stratum components. Under explicit allocation-copy limit conditions for the same absolute-imbalance rule, the sampling-calibrated procedure, SIGA-S, consistently estimates the repeated-sampling variance at a marginal mean- or risk-difference boundary. At a nonsharp boundary, the conditional variance of a fixed-score randomization test can differ because the score contains the observed allocation path. To characterize this distinction, we express the first-order variance gap as a quadratic form involving pair-path covariance and introduce the randomization-calibrated procedure, SIGA-R, based on a reusable paired allocation-only calibration to approximate the conditional reference distribution. Separate comprehensive benchmarks showed close agreement between each SIGA procedure and the corresponding reference randomization test. A trial-inspired simulation based on published aggregate planning characteristics likewise produced similar power for SIGA-S, SIGA-R and the reference randomization test, while both reusable calibration procedures substantially reduced computation relative to nested rerandomization.

</details>


### [177] [Two base rates, two weights: base-rate neglect has a second axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05658)
*Adam Y. Shavit*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 基础比率忽视并非单一错误，而是涉及两种独立的校正：对先验概率的低估（经典基础比率忽视）和对线索本身频率的低估（线索密度效应）。本文用贝叶斯框架中的两个权重统一描述这两种忽视，指出它们在不同任务范式下有不同表现，并预测二者可被双重分离地操控，这是单参数模型无法解释的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将基础比率忽视视为单一错误——对先验赋予过低权重。但作者指出，将观察到的共现转化为有效判断需要同时校正两个基础比率：结果的先验概率和线索本身的频率。线索密度效应在偶然性学习中已被长期研究，但从未被识别为一种基础比率忽视。作者旨在用一个统一的贝叶斯框架揭示这两种忽视的本质联系与区别。

Method: 作者将两种校正表达为一个贝叶斯方程中的两个权重参数。通过分析不同任务范式（分级评分 vs. 二选一测试）下两个权重的可测性，揭示线索频率权重仅在分级评分中显现，而在二选一测试中被抵消。进一步推导了两个权重在极端情况下与信号检测论标准、敏感性/效度/邻接性三元组、因果强度"lift"量度等经典概念的对应关系，并分析了六个标准学习与记忆模型中线索频率权重的隐含位置。

Result: 框架的核心预测是两种忽视可被独立操控——实验者可移动其中一个而不影响另一个，即双重分离。这是任何单参数理论无法产生的模式。该预测尚未被直接检验。作者指出了需要什么样的分级评分实验来验证这一框架。

Conclusion: 基础比率忽视应被理解为两种可分离的校正失败：对先验的忽视和对线索频率的忽视。二者统一于一个双权重贝叶斯框架中。该框架预测双重分离，这是其核心可检验主张，有待后续实验验证。随附论文将两个权重拟合到现有颜色-风味数据集上。

Abstract: Base-rate neglect is usually treated as one mistake: giving the prior too little weight. Turning the co-occurrences you see into a useful judgment, though, means correcting for two base rates, not one. The first is the familiar prior, how common the outcome is. The second is how common the cue itself is. Those are two separate mistakes, and a learner can make either one alone. Under-correcting the prior is classical base-rate neglect; under-correcting the cue is the cue-density effect of contingency learning, long studied but not previously recognised as a kind of base-rate neglect. We write both corrections as two weights in one Bayesian equation. The task decides which weight it can measure: the cue-frequency weight appears only in graded ratings, because a two-choice test cancels it. At their extremes the two weights recover familiar quantities: base-rate neglect, the signal-detection criterion, the contiguity/sensitivity/validity triple, and the "lift" measure of causal strength. The same cue-frequency weight also sits inside six standard learning-and-memory models; they seem to agree, but only because the usual experiments squeeze the data into a form where they cannot disagree. Above all, the two neglects should be separately manipulable: an experimenter can move one without moving the other. That is a double dissociation, and a one-parameter account cannot produce it. That prediction is the framework's centre, and it has not yet been tested. This paper lays out the framework and the rating experiment that would settle it; a companion paper fits the two weights to an existing colour-flavour dataset.

</details>


### [178] [Multiparametric MRI Radiomics and Machine Learning Framework for Predicting Treatment Response in Glioblastoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05733)
*Suchibrata Patra*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于体素级简约药代动力学模型的DCE-MRI影像组学特征联合MGMT状态，能否区分GBM放化疗后的真性进展(TP)与假性进展(PsP)。随机森林分类器联合DCE-MRI组学与MGMT状态取得最优性能(AUC 0.89)，显著优于传统增强T1成像及单一扩展Tofts模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBM患者放化疗后，TP与PsP在常规增强MRI上表现几乎一致，但二者临床管理策略截然不同，现有常规影像无法可靠区分，亟需一种无创、准确的鉴别方法。

Method: 纳入82例IDH野生型GBM患者(53例TP，29例PsP)，均在放化疗后6个月内出现新增强病灶。对DCE-MRI的对比剂浓度-时间曲线进行逐体素拟合，从5个候选药代动力学模型中通过AIC最小化选择最优模型，生成Ktrans、Ve、Vp、taui参数图。分割后提取1073个影像组学特征，经Mann-Whitney U滤波和Elastic Net降维，在四种特征配置下训练五种分类器。

Result: 随机森林分类器联合简约DCE-MRI组学特征与MGMT状态表现最优：平均AUC 0.89，灵敏度0.93，特异度0.76，F1值0.90。优于不含MGMT的组学特征(AUC 0.84)、T1增强基线(AUC 0.72)及单一扩展Tofts模型(AUC 0.68)。Ktrans图的形状和纹理特征以及肿瘤体积是最强预测因子，MGMT提供独立的小幅增量贡献。

Conclusion: 允许体素级模型自适应变化的简约PK建模策略通过更好地捕捉肿瘤局部异质性，提升了DCE-MRI影像组学对TP与PsP的无创鉴别能力，联合MGMT状态可进一步优化分类性能。

Abstract: Distinguishing True Progression (TP) from Pseudo-Progression (PsP) after chemoradiotherapy remains a major diagnostic challenge in GBM, as both entities present near-identical appearances on conventional contrast-enhanced post-treatment MRI. This distinction carries substantial clinical weight, since TP and PsP demand divergent management yet cannot be reliably separated on routine imaging alone. We investigated whether radiomic features derived from a parsimonious, voxel-wise pharmacokinetic model of dynamic contrast-enhanced (DCE) MRI, combined with MGMT status, could discriminate between the two. The cohort comprised 82 adults with IDH-wildtype GBM who developed a new contrast-enhancing lesion within six months of chemoradiotherapy; classification (53 TP, 29 PsP) was established by histopathology where available (n=52) and modified RANO criteria otherwise (n=30). At every voxel, contrast-concentration time courses were fitted to five candidate pharmacokinetic models, and the best fit was retained by AIC minimisation, yielding parsimonious Ktrans, Ve, Vp, and taui maps adapting to local heterogeneity rather than a single fixed model across the tumour. Following segmentation, 1,073 radiomic descriptors were extracted and reduced via Mann-Whitney U filtering and Elastic Net, then used to train five classifiers across four feature configurations. A Random Forest classifier combining parsimonious DCE-MRI radiomics with MGMT status achieved the best discrimination (mean AUC 0.89, sensitivity 0.93, specificity 0.76, F1 0.90), outperforming features without MGMT (0.84), a T1-post-contrast baseline (0.72), and a single-model extended-Tofts analysis (0.68). Shape and textural descriptors of the Ktrans map, with tumour volume, were the strongest predictors, MGMT contributing a smaller, independent effect. Allowing the model to vary voxel-wise improves non-invasive discrimination.

</details>


### [179] [Fuzzy network jump models for soft dynamic clustering of graph-structured data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05786)
*Federico P. Cortese*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种模糊网络跳跃模型，用于对加权图节点上的时间变化观测进行聚类，通过时空正则化实现平滑软聚类，在模拟和旧金山交通网络数据上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类方法难以同时处理时间演化数据与图结构约束，需要一种能融合空间（图连接）和时间平滑性的软聚类框架，以捕捉动态网络中观测的渐变模式。

Method: 构建模糊网络跳跃模型，在加权图上引入空间正则化（鼓励相邻节点聚类分配相似）和时间正则化（鼓励相邻时间点聚类分配相似），利用正则项二次结构设计高效的交替优化算法进行参数估计。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在不同空间依赖程度和簇重叠程度下，该方法均能准确恢复真实隶属概率，且优于其他竞争聚类方法；在旧金山交通网络数据中，识别出可解释的交通状态及其在时间和相连路段上的演化规律。

Conclusion: 所提模型有效整合了图空间信息与时间动态，为网络化时空数据的软聚类提供了灵活且高效的解决方案，兼具理论优势和实际应用价值。

Abstract: We introduce a fuzzy network jump model for clustering time-varying observations indexed by the nodes of a weighted graph. The framework allows flexible graph representations with spatial and temporal regularization promoting smooth soft cluster assignments across connected nodes and consecutive time points. Estimation is performed through an efficient alternating optimization scheme that exploits the quadratic structure of the regularization terms. A simulation study covering different levels of spatial dependence and cluster overlap shows that the proposed method accurately recovers the true membership probabilities and outperforms competing clustering methods. An application to traffic-network data for the city of San Francisco identifies interpretable traffic regimes and reveals their evolution over time and across connected road segments.

</details>


### [180] [Joint Multiple Imputation of Node Attributes and Network Ties in R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05895)
*Robert W. Krause*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: netimpute是一个R包，通过链式方程（netmice）联合插补网络数据中的缺失节点属性和缺失网络关系，利用网络结构特征预测属性、利用属性同质性预测关系，实现了属性和关系的协同插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交网络研究中，节点属性和网络关系经常同时存在缺失值。标准多重插补软件（如mice）能处理属性缺失，但缺乏网络结构概念；而专门的网络插补方法通常将关系插补和属性插补视为分离问题。两者割裂导致忽略了属性与网络结构之间的相互依赖信息。

Method: 提出了netimpute R包，核心包含四个模块：(a) 计算广泛的节点级结构指标和属性同质性指标；(b) 通过主成分分析降维，同时可选择性保留假设检验所需的关键原始指标；(c) 采用类MR-QAP的二元回归模型对网络关系进行逐对（dyadic）建模；(d) 通过netmice()链式方程在属性插补与关系插补之间迭代循环——用网络衍生指标预测属性，用属性衍生指标（及其他网络衍生指标）预测关系。关系更新采用逐条Gibbs采样，每次重抽一条缺失关系后立即通过变化统计量更新内生统计量（互惠性、共享联系人）。额外支持结构零、网络间逻辑约束、社会关系模型随机截距、自定义交互项插补模型，以及网络感知的逐目标预测变量选择（netquickpred()）。

Result: 该包支持二值和加权网络的多类结构指标计算，提供了可复现示例展示其功能。netmice()实现了属性与网络关系的联合插补，相比分离式方法能更好利用网络结构信息与属性同质性信息之间的互补性。

Conclusion: netimpute通过将网络结构信息纳入属性插补、将属性信息纳入关系插补，并采用逐条更新与变化统计量维持内生性结构，提供了一套统一且灵活的网络缺失数据联合插补框架，填补了现有工具在网络与属性协同插补方面的空白。

Abstract: Missing data in social network studies routinely affects both node-level attributes and the network ties themselves. Standard multiple imputation software such as mice (van Buuren and Groothuis-Oudshoorn, 2011) handles the former well but has no notion of network structure, while dedicated network-imputation procedures typically treat tie imputation and attribute imputation as separate problems. This paper introduces netimpute, an R package that (a) computes a broad battery of node-level structural and attribute homophily measures across one or more networks, (b) can reduce these to a manageable set of principal component predictors while optionally preserving specific raw measures that are themselves part of substantive hypotheses, (c) provides a dyadic (cell-level) regression for network ties in the spirit of MR-QAP (Krackhardt, 1988), and (d) combines both into netmice(), a chained-equations routine that jointly imputes missing node attributes and missing network ties by cycling between them, using network-derived predictors for attributes and attribute-derived (and other-network-derived) predictors for ties. Network ties are updated tie-wise by default: a sequential Gibbs step redraws each missing tie one at a time, conditional on all previously imputed ties, refreshing the endogenous statistics (reciprocity, shared contacts) after every single draw via change statistics. The package additionally supports structural zeros, logical constraints between networks, social-relations-model random intercepts in the tie model, custom imputation models with interactions among any internally created terms, and network-aware per-target predictor selection (netquickpred()). We describe the package's design choices, the measures it computes for binary and non-negative weighted networks, and illustrate its use with a reproducible example

</details>


### [181] [A Regression-Based Framework for the ACF, PACF, Durbin-Levinson Recursion, and One-Step-Ahead Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06334)
*Kellen Gong,Fang Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回归的统一教学框架，将自相关函数(ACF)、偏自相关函数(PACF)、Durbin-Levinson递推和一步预测重新解释为普通最小二乘回归的延伸，旨在弥合回归课程与时间序列课程之间的概念鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACF和PACF通常被当作计算程序教给学生，与回归框架脱节。作者希望利用学生已有的回归知识，通过统一的回归视角来降低理解时间序列模型识别工具的门槛。

Method: 将ACF重新定义为平稳过程对其自身滞后项进行简单线性回归的系数；将PACF定义为扩展多元回归模型中最新滞后项的系数；将Durbin-Levinson递推解释为一系列偏回归，每一步都可用普通最小二乘恒等式表达。通过MA(1)和AR(1)的工作示例演示该框架。

Result: 从回归视角出发，ACF和PACF的拖尾与截尾行为可以直接推导和理解。该框架使得时间序列模型识别的核心工具与基础回归课程中已掌握的知识无缝衔接。

Conclusion: 该回归本位教学法降低了回归课程与时间序列课程之间的概念壁垒，为课堂教学提供了具体建议，有助于学生更直观地掌握ARMA模型识别工具的本质。

Abstract: The autocorrelation function (ACF) and partial autocorrelation function (PACF) are foundational tools for identifying autoregressive moving-average (ARMA) models, yet they are often introduced to students as computational recipes disconnected from the regression framework students already know. This note develops a unified, regression-based instructional framework for the ACF, the PACF, the Durbin-Levinson recursion, and one-step-ahead prediction in stationary time series. We show that the ACF is precisely the coefficient from a simple linear regression of a stationary process on one of its own lags, that the PACF is the coefficient on the newest lag in an expanding multiple regression model, and that the Durbin-Levinson recursion is nothing more than a sequence of partial regressions, each expressible using ordinary least-squares identities from a first course in regression. Worked examples for the MA(1) and AR(1) processes illustrate how the tailing-off and cutting-off behavior of the ACF and PACF emerge directly from this viewpoint. We argue that this framing lowers the conceptual barrier between regression courses and time series courses and provide classroom-oriented suggestions for instruction.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Quality Diversity for Reliable Data Driven Time-Use Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05230)
*Aneta Neumann,Ty Stanford,Dorothea Dumuid,Frank Neumann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合预测不确定性的质量多样性（QD）优化框架，用于生成更可靠的时间使用推荐。该框架利用成分数据分析从大规模儿童队列数据中建模日常活动组合与多项健康指标的关系，将预测不确定性嵌入优化过程，在保持高质量解的同时偏向低不确定性区域，从而提供兼顾预期健康收益与置信度的稳健行为健康决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间使用优化方法仅关注最大化预期健康收益，忽视了数据驱动预测中固有的不确定性，这可能导致不切实际的时间使用推荐。因此，作者旨在填补这一空白，将预测不确定性纳入优化框架，以提升推荐的可靠性。

Method: 基于儿童队列数据集（n>1000），采用成分数据分析（CoDA）构建目标函数，捕捉日常活动组合与多项健康指标（BMI、生活满意度、认知等）的关系。提出一种将预测不确定性融入质量多样性（Quality Diversity, QD）过程的新方法，通过基于变量和基于目标的行为表征来探索解空间，揭示不确定性下高质量时间使用组合及健康结果间的显式关系。

Result: 该框架成功将时间使用推荐引导至预测不确定性较低的区域，同时保持了解的高质量结构。通过QD探索，揭示了多样化的高质量时间使用组合，并明确了不确定性条件下健康结果之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，能够在保持高质量解结构的同时向低不确定性区域偏移，从而为行为健康领域提供更可靠、更稳健的时间使用决策支持。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [183] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的协变量调整因果推断框架，用于估计"结局排序合意性"（DOOR）概率以进行获益-风险评估。该框架将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的双线性泛函，通过序贯风险集风险估计条件有序分布，并推导了DOOR概率的有效影响函数。模拟研究表明，结合Super Learner的TMLE（TMLE-SL）在点估计和推断方面表现最优，尤其是交叉拟合版本（CVTMLE-SL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机试验和观察性研究中，获益-风险评估需要对有序结局进行综合分析。DOOR（Desirability of Outcome Ranking）是一种将多个结局整合为有序分类的方法，但现有方法缺乏统一、高效的协变量调整因果推断框架来估计DOOR概率，且未充分利用半参数效率理论进行稳健推断。

Method: 将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序分布的双线性泛函；采用序贯风险集（sequential risk-set）方法估计条件有序结局分布；推导DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）；通过模拟比较G-computation、归一化IPW、AIPW和TMLE四种估计量，结合广义线性模型或Super Learner估计冗余函数；评估含/不含交叉拟合的EIF推断表现（偏倚、标准误准确性、置信区间覆盖率）。

Result: TMLE-SL在点估计中表现最强且最一致，AIPW-SL位居第二；CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏倚、有序分布恢复、标准误准确性和置信区间覆盖率方面整体表现最优；该方法在抗菌药物耐药领导力小组的多重耐药菌网络数据中得到了实证说明。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架为DOOR概率的估计和推断提供了高效、稳健的方法，其中CVTMLE-SL因其双重稳健性和良好的有限样本性质，应作为首选方法应用于实际获益-风险评估中。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>


### [184] [Deep Generalised Mixed Models: a Novel Neural Network Structure for Analysing Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05930)
*Nina van Gerwen,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Manon Hillegers,Loes Keijsers,Sten Willemsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型神经网络架构——深度广义混合模型（Deep Generalised Mixed Model），将混合效应模型推广到深度学习框架中，以解决经验采样法（ESM）纵向数据中高维性和非随机缺失（MAR）带来的偏差问题，并在GrowIt!青少年情绪研究及模拟数据上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于GrowIt!应用中收集的青少年日常情绪ESM数据。传统统计方法难以扩展到高维场景，而标准机器学习方法因参与者因先前强烈负面情绪退出导致的非随机缺失（MAR）而产生选择偏差。现有流程无法妥善处理这类缺失机制。

Method: 开发了将混合效应模型推广到深度学习的新型神经网络架构，通过固定效应和随机效应实现均值和相关结构的半参数灵活建模。估计方法采用变分自编码器（VAE）的改进版本与贝叶斯数据增强算法相结合，可适应任意分布的纵向结局变量，并能在MAR条件下提供有效推断。

Result: 在GrowIt!研究和多项模拟中应用该模型，结果显示了深度广义混合模型的潜力，但由于模型不稳定性，表现未达最优。

Conclusion: 深度广义混合模型为处理含非随机缺失的高维纵向ESM数据提供了有前景的分析框架，但模型稳定性问题仍需进一步解决。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [185] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: PRTrees被扩展为在树构建过程中直接处理缺失预测变量，无需预先插补。提出三种策略：均匀概率法、部分观测法和降维平滑法。实验表明，填充策略对预测性能的影响通常大于平滑分布或代理选择准则，在缺失率较高的数据集上常优于CART。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归树在遇到缺失值时通常需要预先插补，这引入了额外的假设和偏差。PRTrees虽然已具备概率分配的平滑特性，但原框架未直接支持缺失值处理。本文旨在将PRTree框架扩展为原生支持缺失协变量，消除对插补步骤的依赖，同时保持概率守恒和边际相容性等核心性质。

Method: 提出三种缺失值处理策略：(1) 均匀概率法——将缺失维度上的分裂概率均匀分配；(2) 部分观测法——仅基于观测到的维度计算分裂概率，忽略缺失维度；(3) 降维平滑法——在观测维度的子空间上构建平滑分布。三种方法均被设计为保证概率守恒和边际相容性，适用于任意缺失模式。

Result: 在多个不同缺失水平的真实数据集上评估，结果表明：(1) 缺失值处理策略对概率树构建效果影响显著；(2) 填充策略比平滑分布或代理选择准则对预测性能的影响更大；(3) 在缺失比例较高的数据集上，所提方法常优于经典CART。

Conclusion: PRTrees可被有效扩展以原生处理缺失值，填充策略是主导建模因素。该方法在保持树模型可解释性和灵活性的同时，在高缺失率场景下实现了优于传统回归树的预测性能。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [186] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为随机局部共形预测（RLCP）提供了首个有限样本理论保证：在Hölder正则性条件下，证明了条件覆盖缺口和长度误差相对于oracle的高概率一致上界，并分析了数据分割学习得分下的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测理论仅提供边际覆盖的有限样本保证，而RLCP虽能改善局部条件覆盖，但缺乏对其实现后的局部集合在条件有效性和oracle效率方面的有限样本联合控制理论。

Method: 在固定得分情形下，假设条件得分CDF满足Hölder正则性及标准密度与核假设，推导局部邻域内一致的条件覆盖缺口和长度误差的高概率界；进而分析数据分割学习得分（如共形化分位数回归），将局部保证分解为固定得分校准误差与一致得分估计误差之和。

Result: 所得界分解为O(h^β)的局部化偏差和随校准集大小递减的校准项，清晰刻画了带宽的偏差-方差权衡；在学习得分情形下，改进学习质量可直接收紧局部保证。

Conclusion: 本文首次为RLCP提供了有限样本下条件有效性与oracle效率的联合理论保证，明确了带宽选择的核心权衡，并将局部保证与得分学习质量建立起定量联系。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [187] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化分类中梯度下降的早停策略。在含标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，早停可克服最大间隔插值分类器的统计次优性，达到极小极大最优的零一风险；中心技术贡献是一个新的校准结果，可将对数损失转化为零一损失，并去除了标准界中的平方根速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在过参数化分类中，即使数据真实分布非线性可分，训练数据也可线性分离。此时梯度下降在逻辑损失上范数发散但方向收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，其隐式偏差在统计上可能是次优的。作者旨在证明早停可以克服这一缺陷，达到统计最优。

Method: 在含标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，分析梯度下降早停在oracle时间点的行为。方法包含两部分：(1) 建立早停迭代点的尖锐上界；(2) 建立任意分类器的匹配统计下界，二者共同给出最优收敛速率。核心技术贡献是一个新的校准界，能在模型误设（由标签翻转噪声引起）下将对数损失超额风险转化为零一损失超额风险，并消除标准校准界中的平方根速率。

Result: 对于具有快速连续衰减协方差谱（包括多项式衰减和指数衰减）的高斯混合模型，早停在合适的oracle时间可达到极小极大最优的零一超额风险。同时证明了线性插值分类器为达到相同超额风险，所需样本量可能是指数级多于早停所需的样本量。实验结果验证了理论速率。

Conclusion: 早停是对抗过参数化分类中最大间隔插值分类器统计次优性的有效手段，在标签噪声场景下可达到极小极大最优速率。新的校准结果独立具有重要性，适用于模型误设情形并改进了标准界。线性插值器在统计效率上可能远逊于早停。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [188] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于强化学习的持久图（PD）空间上的随机动力学框架，将PD视为可通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作演化的动态对象，建立了诱导马尔可夫链的不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性条件，并设计了兼顾分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计和结构保持压缩的目标函数，实现自适应拓扑简化和概率建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献通常将持久图视为静态对象，缺乏对PD空间上概率建模和随机演化的框架支持，限制了其在动态拓扑数据分析中的应用。

Method: 在可变基数的有限PD空间上，通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义受控马尔可夫过程；建立诱导马尔可夫链的不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性的理论条件，证明唯一平稳概率律的存在性；设计包含分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计量和结构保持压缩的奖励函数，采用强化学习引导动力学过程。

Result: 在合成数据和神经影像持久图上的实验表明，该框架能够在显著降低图复杂度的同时，保持主导拓扑结构的完整性。

Conclusion: 该框架为PD空间提供了统一的概率建模与自适应拓扑简化方法，弥合了拓扑数据分析与随机过程之间的鸿沟，具有理论和应用双重价值。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [189] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Larsen 等人提出的单调对手插入模型，证明在 VC 维 d≥2 时，minimax 期望误差为 Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，额外对数因子是固有的，而非算法缺陷。同时，用 Littlestone 维替代 VC 维也成立，表明在线到批处理的 O(d_L/n) 速率不可达。d=1 时上界为 Θ(1/n)，由一个基于留一法的不当学习器实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen 等人此前在单调对手插入模型下证明 ERM 可达 O((d/n)log(n/d)) 误差，并质疑额外对数因子究竟是特定算法的人为产物，还是缺乏可交换性的内在后果。本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 通过一个统一的构造进行下界证明：构造一个显式类及其先验，使得两个仅在非可忽略质量点上不同的目标假设产生完全相同的样本分布。该构造同时适用于 VC 维和 Littlestone 维。上界方面，对 d=1 采用基于留一法（leave-one-out）和单包含图（one-inclusion graph）的不当学习器。

Result: 对于 d≥2，minimax 期望误差为 Θ((d/n)log(n/d))；d=1 时为 Θ(1/n)。以 Littlestone 维 d_L 替代 d 得到相同速率，证明干净的在线到批处理速率 O(d_L/n) 不可达。添加正确标记的样本反而使学习难度增加一个对数因子。

Conclusion: 额外对数因子是缺乏可交换性导致的固有代价，而非特定算法局限。这一反直觉结果表明，即使是正确标记的对抗性插入也会在 VC 维 ≥2 时导致更慢的学习速率，且这种困难在在线学习设置下同样存在。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [190] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的VARMA模型估计框架，通过偏自相关重参数化、Parseval恒等式构建固定规模充分统计量，使每次迭代成本与序列长度T无关，解决了高维VARMA似然估计的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型虽能用少量参数捕捉移动平均项（而纯VAR需大量滞后），但长期以来被认为在中高维度下不实用，原因有三：似然非凸、参数化仅在等价类下可识别、每次评估需遍历整个序列（计算成本与T成正比）。

Method: 提出三管齐下的估计框架：(1) 偏自相关重参数化，从构造上保证平稳性和可逆性；(2) 对重参数化系数施加高斯先验，对角与非对角元素采用不同尺度；(3) 利用Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式，以近线性截断代价将数据依赖压缩为固定规模的充分统计量。由此得到两种点估计：正则化最小二乘拟合和协方差边际化的最大后验估计。该框架可直接扩展至季节性动态、外生回归变量（VARMAX）和滚动窗口重拟合。

Result: 理论上证明了在固定维度下，两种估计量均以近参数速率恢复真实过程的无穷自回归表示，截断不引入渐近偏差。实证上，从d=10到d=40，估计量均接近Oracle预测误差（此时经典条件MLE产生不可逆拟合导致预测发散）；在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据上匹敌或超越VAR、贝叶斯VAR、分量ARMA和稀疏VARMA等基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架将基于似然的VARMA估计带入了此前从业者只能依赖VAR模型的问题规模，每次迭代成本与序列长度无关，消除了VARMA在实际应用中的计算障碍。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting多智能体协作框架，将遗留企业应用封装为自主AI智能体，通过分层嵌套结构实现跨应用的自然语言交互与流程编排，即"应用即智能体"和"对话即集成"的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业依赖大量异构业务系统，导致严重的数据孤岛和流程碎片化。传统集成方案（ESB、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升、智能能力不足等固有限制，亟需一种更灵活、智能的企业应用集成方法。

Method: 提出Agentic Nesting框架：(1)将每个遗留应用封装为自主AI智能体，提取数字代理以实现自然语言交互与自主操控；(2)按分层管理拓扑组织智能体，反映企业生态系统的组合复杂性；(3)通过中央编排器进行任务分解与动态调度；(4)暴露统一对话界面，实现跨应用查询与流程编排。

Result: 提出了"应用即智能体"（Application-as-Agent）集成范式与"对话即集成"（Conversation-as-Integration）交互理念，并探索了该方法在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting框架通过将企业应用转化为层级嵌套的AI智能体体系，以自然语言对话为统一交互入口，有望克服传统集成方案的根本性缺陷，为异构企业系统协调和大规模数据应用提供一种更具智能化和泛化能力的集成路径。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [192] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出点火指数（Ignition Index, I），一个基于四参数S型函数拟合的标量度量，用于在Transformer语言模型中操作化全局工作空间理论（GWT）的“全或无”点火预测。该指标在11个模型、5个架构家族中验证，展现出9.6倍的对真实语言结构的选择性，揭示了前馈Transformer与SSM之间、循环架构中迭代轴与深度轴之间的显著点火差异，并发现了训练过程中的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏一种可量化的方法来桥接全局工作空间理论（GWT）的“全或无”点火动力学预测与大规模语言模型的机制可解释性研究。现有文献中缺少能跨架构、跨训练阶段进行系统性比较的标准化指标。

Method: 提出点火指数（I）：将每层线性探针精度作为输入信号强度的函数，拟合四参数S型曲线，提取陡度参数β̂。高β̂值表示突变的点火式相变，低β̂值表示渐进式积累。通过打乱标签对照实验评估选择性。在11个模型（涵盖5个架构家族）上进行验证，使用Mann-Whitney U检验和Cohen's d进行统计比较，并采用PELT算法检测训练过程中的相变点。

Result: (1) 前馈Transformer的聚合β̂比SSM高89%（p<1e-13, Cohen's d=0.52），Mamba呈现近乎线性的曲线，表明缺乏全局广播机制；(2) Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点火强度是深度轴的2.12倍；(3) Pythia-410M在训练步256处检测到相变（+67%），早于归纳头形成；(4) 模型规模和信号强度与点火指数的关联假设未得到支持，提示Transformer架构可能已饱和其可用点火机制。

Conclusion: 点火指数首次提供了GWT动力学预测与机制可解释性之间的有效量化桥梁，具有9.6倍的测量选择性和架构级别的区分能力，这些特性在缩放文献中此前未被表征。该指标为理解语言模型中类似意识的全局信息整合机制开辟了新的实证研究路径。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [193] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在推理任务上的失败常源于中间步骤的局部推理错误，而非全局能力不足。本文发现这些错误可通过弱模型生成的短补丁修复，并提出Woodpecker Distillation——一种弱到强的训练框架，通过对比局部干预来蒸馏纠正信号，在数学推理基准上持续提升强模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型尽管具备解决推理任务的能力，却经常失败。作者认为失败根源在于中间步骤的局部推理错误（localized reasoning bugs），而非全局能力缺陷。关键洞察是：弱模型生成的补丁可以修复强模型的推理轨迹，但直接微调无法可靠地内化这种纠正效果——有用的信号不在于干预文本本身，而在于它如何重塑模型未来的推理分布。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation（啄木鸟蒸馏），一种弱到强训练框架，核心思路是利用对比局部干预进行学习：（1）在相同推理前缀处，对比成功与失败的弱模型补丁；（2）从两者诱导的未来token预测中构建纠正性教师分布（corrective teacher distribution）；（3）将该分布信号蒸馏到强模型中。该方法不同于直接模仿补丁文本，而是蒸馏干预对推理分布的塑造作用。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation能够持续提升强模型的推理性能，并且优于直接模仿基线方法（direct imitation baselines），验证了从对比局部干预中蒸馏纠正信号的有效性。

Conclusion: 局部推理错误是可修复的，修复的关键在于学习干预如何改变推理分布而非干预文本本身。Woodpecker Distillation通过对比局部干预蒸馏纠正信号，为弱到强训练提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [194] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨在急性缺血性卒中90天预后预测中，用临床指南对齐的离散化分类变量替代连续预测因子，结果显示在全分类梯度提升模型中，两个治疗队列的预测性能与连续变量模型无统计显著差异，且全局特征重要性排名保持一致，表明基于指南的离散化是一种可行的模型设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天预后预测中表现优异，但模型解释与临床医生推理之间存在错位，限制了临床采纳。受一项临床医生用户研究的启发，该研究呼吁采用与临床指南对齐的截断值，作者提出研究问题：能否在不牺牲预测性能的前提下，将连续预测因子替换为临床信息驱动的分类编码。

Method: 在欧洲多中心注册数据上，按三种治疗队列（treatment cohorts）分层。对比标准梯度提升模型与全分类梯度提升模型，后者采用与卒中指南对齐、治疗特异性的阈值进行离散化。使用统计检验比较预测准确性，并分析全局特征重要性排名的一致性。

Result: 在两个治疗队列中，全分类模型与连续变量模型的预测性能在统计上不可区分；在一个队列中出现预测准确性的显著下降。全局特征重要性排名在两种模型间保持一致，表明离散化保留了预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于指南的类别化是卒中预后模型的一种可行的设计选择，能够在保持预测性能的同时提升模型解释与临床推理的对齐程度。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [195] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE 是一个面向 LLM-agent 技能复用的多轨迹溯源审计框架，通过提取表达式、实现和操作三种溯源轨迹，并构建技能操作图（SOG）来捕捉运行结构，在基准测试中取得了 AUROC 0.938 和 F1 0.898 的成绩，并在大规模实践中展现出超越仓库级别基线的审计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM-agent 生态系统中可复用技能的快速增长，技能作为包含元数据、自然语言指令、代码、工具和操作流程的混合模态制品，其复用审计问题已不同于传统代码克隆检测。现有检测器仅针对单一模态源代码或整体包相似度，无法捕捉分布在文本、实现片段和操作结构中的复用证据，容易遗漏仅保留技能某一组成部分的复用行为。

Method: SKILLTRACE 框架提取三种溯源轨迹：表达式轨迹（Expression Trace）、实现轨迹（Implementation Trace）和操作轨迹（Operational Trace）。其中，操作轨迹被建模为技能操作图（SOG），捕获激活、过程和资源流结构。LLM 仅在摄入阶段辅助操作轨迹的提取，审计时采用确定性方式比对已缓存的轨迹，并针对同功能的严格负样本进行校准，最终报告哪些轨迹支持复用判断。

Result: 在 SKILLTRACE-BENCH 上（含 820 个变换后的复用正例基于 100 个市场锚技能，以及 751 个负对照），SKILLTRACE 达到 AUROC 0.938 和 F1 0.898。在 36,446 个技能的野生审计中，基于轨迹归属的证据能够生成超越仓库级别基线的可操作复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE 通过多模态、多轨迹的溯源方式有效解决了 LLM-agent 技能复用审计问题，展示出在技能市场中检测跨模态、部分复用行为的显著优势，为技能制品的溯源治理提供了实用的技术方案。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [196] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR），将具体因果对泛化为关系层级的抽象因果逻辑，通过多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统结合相似性约束聚类构建AECR知识库，并设计抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE）将其注入事件预测任务，显著提升了低频长尾和未见事件组合上的因果推理泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于实例级因果对的方法在低频长尾事件和未见事件组合上泛化能力严重不足，制约了事件驱动智能分析系统在风险预警、决策支持和叙事理解中的实际应用效果。

Method: 首先提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR）的概念框架，将具体因果对转化为关系层级的抽象因果逻辑；其次设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统，配合相似性约束聚类从噪声数据中蒸馏可信的AECR，并构建两个完整的AECR知识库；最后提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE），通过规则引导注意力层与门控表示融合机制，将检索到的AECR注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）基准任务。

Result: 定量实验表明，引入AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，在事件预测任务上带来一致的性能提升，尤其在稀有和未见事件样本上的增益最为突出。

Conclusion: 抽象事件因果规则作为一种关系层级的因果抽象范式，能够有效弥补实例级因果对泛化能力不足的缺陷，为事件中心智能分析系统提供更稳健的因果知识支撑。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [197] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter 是一个仅有 1530 万参数的象棋 AI，通过将下棋建模为时间感知的序列过程来预测人类走法。它结合了走法历史编码器和时间控制模块，在 6.1B 位置上训练，以 55.23% 的 top-1 准确率超越了 Maia 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类象棋行为模型（如 Maia 2）通常将每个局面视为独立快照进行预测，忽略了人类下棋是一个具有时序依赖性的过程——棋手的开局偏好、局势漂移和局内行为倾向会随对局进程演变，同时时钟压力也会显著影响走法选择。作者旨在通过显式建模时间维度与序列上下文来更准确地预测人类走法。

Method: Otter 采用两个核心条件信号：(1) 走法历史编码器（move history encoder），以最近 20 步走法作为条件输入，捕捉开局偏好、局面漂移和局内行为倾向；(2) 时间控制模块（time control module），根据时钟压力调节预测。模型仅 15.3M 参数，在 1.17 亿局 Lichess 快棋对局（共 61 亿个局面）上训练，使用单张 T4 GPU 训练 30 天。

Result: Otter 在走法预测上达到 55.23% 的 top-1 准确率和 90.95% 的 top-5 准确率，超越了此前最先进的人类象棋模型 Maia 2，且参数量和训练数据量均更少。在 11 个 Elo 分段中，准确率在 1900-1999 分段达到峰值 57.38%。

Conclusion: 将象棋建模为时间感知的序列活动比仅基于局面的方法能更准确地预测人类走法，且所需模型规模更小。该工作表明显式融入时序上下文和时钟压力信号对于建模人类决策行为至关重要。代码、模型和训练日志均已开源。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [198] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchAuditBench是一个包含1,243条失败搜索轨迹的基准数据集，用于评估LLM审计器定位、归因和修复深度搜索智能体失败的能力；同时提出SearchAuditor多视角审计框架，在端到端通过率上达到32.3%，优于GPT-5.5等基线的26.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体在长程网页交互中容易因小的推理错误导致最终答案错误，而手动检查极长的执行轨迹来诊断失败非常困难，甚至超出人类能力。因此需要自动化审计工具来定位、归因和修复这些失败，以减轻人工负担。

Method: (1) 构建SearchAuditBench基准，从8个开源模型在5个深度搜索基准上收集1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息、65.1K tokens），每条轨迹由专家标注关键错误步骤、搜索特定根因及参考修复方案与评分标准；(2) 提出SearchAuditor多视角审计框架，通过证据驱动的裁决机制实现失败定位、归因与修复。

Result: 即使使用GPT-5.5等前沿模型的最强基线方法，端到端通过率也仅为26.6%；而SearchAuditor在所有前沿模型上一致优于基线，端到端通过率达到32.3%，且其修复方案可使失败智能体更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: 自动化审计深度搜索智能体的失败轨迹是可行且有效的，SearchAuditor框架能够显著提升审计质量，降低人工诊断成本，并帮助搜索智能体从错误中恢复。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [199] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PD-GS提出了一种音素驱动的高斯泼溅方法，通过在3DGS说话人模型中引入时间对齐的音素token和语言融合模块(LFM)，解决了唇部发音模糊和"漏嘴"伪影问题，在HDTF数据集上取得了最佳的唇部几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS说话人渲染中，唇部运动存在过度平滑和违反硬性发音约束（如双唇闭合）的问题。核心困难在于：简短的离散发音事件是通过连续声学嵌入在回归目标下推断的，导致预测偏向平均口型配置。自我监督语音编码器虽提供丰富的韵律和语音线索，但缺乏显式的、帧对齐的语言学目标来可靠地消歧闭合级事件。

Method: 提出PD-GS（Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting），包含两个关键设计：(1) 利用自动ASR和强制对齐流水线获取时间对齐的音素token，作为3DGS说话人模型的额外输入；(2) 核心组件语言融合模块(LFM)，通过可学习的门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在保持平滑音频驱动动态的同时，在发音关键段上增强音素引导。训练仅使用单目视频，采用图像重建和唇部关键点监督。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在对比基线中取得了最佳唇部几何精度（LMD 2.66），并在具有挑战性的音素序列中定性减少了闭合违规（"漏嘴"伪影），生成了语言学上更忠实的神经化身。

Conclusion: 通过引入时间对齐的音素信息和自适应融合机制，PD-GS有效解决了3DGS说话人渲染中的唇部发音模糊问题，证明了显式语言学知识对于高质量发音渲染的重要性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [200] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMRC-SD 通过状态匹配路由与上下文化自蒸馏，仅在学生当前执行状态与参考轨迹中的状态相匹配时才施加蒸馏，解决了特权蒸馏中状态-参考不匹配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交互式环境中，学生智能体的动作会不断改变执行状态。当学生采取不同动作或以不同顺序完成子目标时，其轨迹可能到达参考轨迹未覆盖的状态，导致特权蒸馏中的状态-参考不匹配（state–reference mismatch），使参考成为不可靠的指导来源。因此需要一个机制，确保特权参考指导与学生当前执行状态兼容。

Method: 提出 SMRC-SD（State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation）方法，包含两个核心机制：（1）状态匹配路由（State-Matched Routing）：在每一轮验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的某个受支持状态匹配，仅在匹配时施加蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏本地兼容指导的轮次；（2）上下文化自蒸馏（Contextualized Self-Distillation）：对每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹中构建状态条件化的教师上下文，将监督建立在学生实际到达的状态之上。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上，Qwen3-1.7B 的任务成功率从 0.746 提升至 0.865；在 WebShop 上，从 0.574 提升至 0.693。受控的路由和上下文消融实验表明，选择局部受支持轮次和构建状态兼容教师上下文均对性能提升有贡献。

Conclusion: SMRC-SD 通过明确判定何时以及如何使用特权轨迹来指导在策略学生，有效解决了状态-参考不匹配问题。结果表明，在策略特权蒸馏中必须考虑学生与参考轨迹之间的状态对齐，仅在全匹配状态下施加蒸馏并使用状态条件化上下文是关键设计。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [201] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文通过训练14个不同规模的模型（135M至14B参数）在Psych-101数据集上分析认知微调模型的缩放特性，发现分布内性能很快饱和（0.6B–1B即可匹配70B），而分布外泛化呈现陡峭的缩放梯度。消融实验表明，刺激与反馈内容贡献了75.7%的学得信息，模型并非仅依赖选择历史的统计捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究以人类行为数据微调的大语言模型作为通用认知代理时，规模是否关键，以及模型究竟在习得任务结构还是利用统计捷径。

Method: 在Psych-101（160个实验、1070万试次选择）上训练4个架构家族、14个规模的模型；通过分布内/分布外评估刻画缩放规律；使用两种诊断工具：逐步遮蔽四种提示通道（任务指令、刺激、反馈、选择历史）和试次顺序置换。

Result: 分布内性能趋于天花板，小模型即可匹配大模型；分布外泛化中规模效应显著放大。遮蔽刺激与反馈内容使75.7%信息丢失、模型降至随机水平以下，证明选择历史无法单独解释性能。置换显示模型对独立试次任务具有不变性，但对试次顺序依赖先前响应的任务敏感。

Conclusion: 小规模认知微调模型有望作为心理实验的噪声上限估计器，但其适用范围受限于训练中见过的范式。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [202] [Innovation-Residual Auditing of Autonomous Analysis Agents: Localization, Detection Limits, Error Control, and Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05490)
*Ahmed Hassoon,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对基于"无标注错误、仅从已知正确分析中学习"的自动化审计方法进行了严格的可靠性研究。核心发现包括：(1)评分方式决定了错误能否被定位——基于前一步操作的评分会使错误无法传播，而基于更长重构的评分会分散错误；(2)给出了在单个分析中控制误报率的程序，仅需正确分析的可交换性；(3)建立了任何此类审计的归因下限：低于某个幅度的错误本质上无法与正常变异区分，且该下限随训练数据增加而下降极慢，表示维度而非数据量是瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主数据分析代理日益普及，当分析出错时需要定位错误来源。近期出现了一种无需标注错误、仅从已知正确分析中学习的方法来标记异常操作，但这类审计的可靠性尚未被系统研究。本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与概率建模相结合的方法。首先分析不同评分策略（基于紧邻操作 vs. 基于长程重构）对错误传播模式的影响，量化错误分散程度；其次，在仅假设正确分析可交换（而非模型完全正确）的条件下，给出控制单次审计中误报率的统计程序；然后分析模型不完美或分析被选择性审查时保证的弱化程度；最后通过信息论推导审计的归因下限。

Result: (1) 基于前一步操作的评分使单一错误产生单一标记，但继承错误不可区分；基于更长重构的评分将单一错误分散到多个操作上。(2) 给出了量化错误分散程度及渐变错误下最优比较长度的方法。(3) 在可交换性假设下给出了误报率控制程序。(4) 当模型不完美或分析被选择性审查时，量化了保证的衰减。(5) 归因下限随正确分析数量增加而下降极为缓慢——在当前表示规模下，增加百倍数据仅降低不到2%，表明表示维度是核心约束。

Conclusion: 基于无标注错误学习的审计方法的可靠性受评分策略、模型质量和审查选择机制显著影响。最重要的瓶颈在于表示维度而非训练数据量，这为该领域的未来发展指明了方向：提升表示能力比单纯收集更多数据更关键。

Abstract: Autonomous agents now carry out entire data analyses, selecting cohorts, joining tables, and fitting models with little step-by-step supervision. When such an analysis turns out to be wrong, someone must determine which operation caused it. A recent approach does this without any labelled mistakes, learning instead from analyses known to be sound and flagging operations that depart from what that model predicts; how reliable such audits are has not been studied. This paper supplies that analysis. The choice of score determines whether an error can be localized at all. If each operation is scored by how surprising it is given the operation immediately preceding it, then operations that merely inherit an earlier error are indistinguishable from correct ones, so one mistake produces one flag; scores computed against a longer reconstruction of the intended analysis instead spread a single mistake across many operations. We quantify how far they spread, and how to choose the comparison length when an error accumulates gradually rather than at once. We then give procedures that control the proportion of falsely flagged operations within a single audited analysis, requiring only that sound analyses be exchangeable rather than that the fitted model be correct, and we quantify how much the guarantees weaken when the model is imperfect or when the analysis was selected for review in a way that depends on its content. Finally we establish a limit on what any such audit can report: errors below a certain magnitude cannot be attributed at all, being indistinguishable from ordinary variation among sound analyses. This limit falls so slowly as more sound analyses are collected that at the representation sizes now in use a hundredfold increase reduces it by under two percent, so the dimension of the representation rather than the volume of training data is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [203] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一种基于有向谱系图的项目级规划层，用于将研究项目自动分解为边界清晰、依赖明确、可执行的任务组合，显著提升了任务质量和下游执行精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单任务系统将研究项目视为单一超大任务，产出模糊、重叠的扁平任务集，或依赖人工协调任务边界与执行顺序，缺乏自动化的项目到任务的结构化分解能力。研究项目本质上是长周期议程，需要通过多个有界任务推进，这些任务具有不同但相关的目标、并行替代方案和依赖感知序列。

Method: 提出Project2Task规划层：(1) 将候选贡献表示为"创新原子"(innovation atoms)，并组织在有向谱系图(directed lineage graph)中；(2) 使用轻量级伯努利块模型(Bernoulli block-model)目标函数，在水平、垂直和混合组合分解中进行选择；(3) 生成具有明确贡献归属的有界任务，修复重叠和缺失的执行字段；(4) 输出依赖感知的任务合约，包含目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖关系和执行顺序。合约独立于下游执行器。

Result: 在10个项目简报（约30个任务）的基准测试中，基于稿件的组合评估显示：Project2Task平均质量得分7.15，显著优于Brief Baseline(4.58)和Topic-only Setting(5.31)。与AutoResearchClaw集成后，下游任务平均准确率从0.536提升至0.759。

Conclusion: 显式的项目到任务规划对于生成连贯、无冗余且可执行的研究任务组合具有重要价值，证明了在自主研究中引入结构化规划层的有效性。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [204] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriQua 提出了一种自适应的事实建模框架，简单声明用标准三元组表示，复杂声明用带限定词的超关系事实表示，以在保持原子性的同时保留必要上下文；验证过程可在三元组和限定词层面直接标注错误，并引入 TriQuaScore 量化事实性评分，实验表明其与人工评分高度一致，且在证据驱动的验证中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"分解-验证"范式面临根本性权衡：原子事实（单句单信息）常丢失关键上下文，而更宽泛的陈述又缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。需要一种既能保留上下文、又不牺牲原子性的灵活事实建模方式。

Method: 提出 TriQua 框架：① 按复杂度自适应建模事实——简单声明提取为标准三元组（主-谓-宾），复杂声明通过附加辅助上下文限定词扩展为超关系事实；② 验证阶段在三元组和限定词层面直接标注具体错误，提供细粒度可解释性；③ 设计 TriQuaScore 量化结构化事实单元的事实性。

Result: TriQuaScore 与人工标注的事实性评分高度一致；TriQua 在分解质量上表现鲁棒；在基于证据的事实验证任务中优于现有基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: TriQua 通过超关系事实的自适应建模有效缓解了原子性与上下文保留之间的张力，其细粒度错误标注和 TriQuaScore 为 LLM 事实性评估提供了更精确、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [205] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV)系统，该系统将书面梦境描述转化为四幅时间序列面板图像，利用大语言模型进行文本切分，再通过文生图模型生成图像，并确保序列间的视觉一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境情感强烈但难以传达；现有的文本到图像生成方法缺乏对梦境叙事时间序列的可视化支持，且难以保持跨图像视觉连贯性。

Method: DSV系统分三步：首先用大语言模型（LLM）将梦境描述按时间顺序拆分为四个部分；然后使用文生图模型（text-to-image model）为每部分生成图像，并通过技术手段保持序列间的视觉一致性；最后对不匹配文本描述的图像进行重新生成（regeneration），并采用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL视觉语言模型进行客观质量、保真度和连贯性评估。

Result: 在DreamBank梦境数据库的50个可视化样本上进行了评估，通过CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL三个视觉语言模型，报告了图像质量（quality）、文本-图像保真度（fidelity）和序列间视觉连贯性（coherence）的客观指标结果。

Conclusion: DSV系统成功实现了从文本梦境描述到时间序列图像可视化的转换，验证了LLM+文生图模型流水线在叙事性、时序性内容可视化方面的可行性。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [206] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Search2Skill 是一个新型框架，通过自动识别 LLM 智能体的能力缺口、检索外部知识源并将检索证据蒸馏为结构化可复用技能，突破了传统自进化技能方法仅依赖模型内部参数知识的局限。该框架由基于评分量表的强化学习方案优化，在八个专家级领域上一致优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化技能构建方法（如从模型参数知识或轨迹中构建技能）受限于模型已有知识边界，而专业领域中的领域惯例和标准流程往往超出这一边界，难以仅靠智能体自身激发出来。因此需要一种能主动检索外部知识并将其转化为可复用技能的方法。

Method: 提出 Search2Skill 框架，核心包括三个自动优化环节：何时检索（when to search）、如何检索（how to search）以及如何生成技能（how to generate skills）。通过基于评分量表（rubric-based）的强化学习方案对这三个环节进行联合优化，最终将检索到的外部证据蒸馏为结构化的可复用技能。

Result: 在三个基准测试的八个专家级领域上，Search2Skill 在流式评估和留出评估两种协议下均一致优于检索增强基线和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。进一步分析表明，性能增益来源于技能抽象而非原始检索证据，且所获技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill 有效突破了 LLM 智能体在专业领域自进化的知识边界瓶颈，通过外部知识检索与技能蒸馏的结合，实现了更优的专家级任务性能，且技能具备跨模型规模的迁移能力。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains.
