Across 13 contemporary LLMs spanning diverse model families and sizes, we find that country identities and user affiliations can systematically affect how equivalent actions are described, evaluated, and defended under international law. Our results thus establish Poli-Bias as a fine-grained framework for auditing political even-handedness and sycophancy in LLMs.

</details>


### [272] [Contextual Information Policy Optimization for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06128)
*Xingyu Guo,Wei Chen,Linlin Yang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIPO（Contextual Information Policy Optimization），一种面向证据的强化学习框架，通过赋予检索信息影响推理步骤的密集轮次级奖励，遏制搜索代理中的先验驱动推理和确认偏误，在7个基准上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理的强化学习主要奖励最终答案正确性或中间进展，未直接评估检索后的推理行为是否真正基于检索证据。这一错位导致"先验驱动推理"——智能体依赖内部知识形成结论，检索仅用于确认，产生确认偏误和低效证据使用。

Method: 提出CIPO框架：1）设计密集的轮次级信用分配机制，奖励受检索信息影响的推理动作；2）将该证据使用信号与全局结果奖励相结合，保持答案正确性；3）无需人工过程标注或额外奖励模型。

Result: 在7个域内和域外基准上的大量实验表明，CIPO降低了先验驱动推理的普遍性，并在大多数任务上取得了优异性能。

Conclusion: CIPO通过显式将对齐策略优化与外部证据使用相结合，有效遏制了搜索代理的确认偏误，促进了检索事实能够引导或修正后续推理的轨迹。

Abstract: Search agents extend large language models beyond static parametric memory by enabling them to acquire and use ex ternal evidence during multi-step reasoning. For knowledge intensive tasks involving complex or evolving information, their reliability depends not only on retrieving relevant ev idence but also on using it to guide subsequent reasoning. However, existing methods primarily reward final-answer cor rectness or intermediate progress, without directly assessing whether post-retrieval actions are grounded in the retrieved evidence. This misalignment encourages prior-driven reason ing: agents form conclusions based on internal knowledge and use retrieval mainly to confirm them, resulting in confirma tion bias and inefficient evidenceuse.Toaddressthisissue, we propose Contextual Information Policy Optimization (CIPO), an evidence-oriented reinforcement learning framework that explicitly aligns policy optimization with external evidence use. CIPO assigns dense, turn-level credit to reasoning ac tions influenced by retrieved information, while combining this evidence-use signal with a global outcome reward to pre serveanswercorrectness.Withthismanner,CIPOdiscourages evidence-detached guesses and promotes reasoning trajecto ries in which retrieved facts can guide or revise subsequent reasoning. Importantly, CIPO requires neither human process annotations nor an additional reward model. Extensive exper iments on seven in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks show that CIPO reduces the prevalence of prior-driven rea soning and achieves excellent performance on most tasks.

</details>


### [273] [Schema-Guided Hierarchical Information Extraction and Semantic Evaluation Using Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06167)
*Modhurita Mitra,Jan-Willem Versteeg,Maarten D. Schermer,Shiva Nadi Najafabadi,Marie L. De Bruin,Lourens T. Bloem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模式（schema）的框架，利用生成式AI从非结构化文本中提取复杂结构化信息，并通过路径语义匹配算法和分级评分标准对提取结果进行自动评估。在NICE发布的HTA文档上，Claude Opus 3模型以人工三十分之一的时间实现了14个属性中12个属性F1>90%的表现，并展示了跨模型、跨机构和跨语言的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非结构化文本（如卫生技术评估HTA报告）中提取复杂的、具有层次嵌套结构和可变基数的结构化信息是一项耗时且依赖人工的任务。需要一个统一、系统化且可自动评估的框架来高效完成此类信息提取。

Method: 提出基于模式的框架：①定义模式作为领域知识的信息模型，统一描述层次化、嵌套属性及其可变基数；②单次调用生成式AI（零样本模式）完成文档信息抽取；③引入基于路径的语义匹配算法对齐抽取结果与金标准中的嵌套属性；④利用生成式AI对属性值进行语义比较，并按领域特定标准将其分类为精确匹配、语义匹配、有用匹配或非匹配四个等级。

Result: 在NICE的HTA文档上，使用Claude Opus 3模型，14个属性中有12个F1分数超过90%；提取时间约为人类领域专家的1/30。框架在不同生成式AI模型（跨模型）、不同HTA机构（跨机构）和不同语言（跨语言）上均展现出良好的泛化性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该基于模式的生成式AI框架能够高效、准确地从非结构化文档中提取复杂嵌套信息，并通过创新的语义评估方法自动验证提取质量，在效率、准确性和泛化能力方面均表现优异，具有广泛的跨领域应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a schema-based framework for extracting complex, structured information from unstructured text documents using generative AI, followed by automated semantic evaluation of the extracted information against a gold standard. The schema, serving as an information model encoding domain knowledge, provides a unified, systematic, and consistent framework for extraction of hierarchical, nested information, with attributes of variable cardinality, and subsequent evaluation of the results. Information extraction from a document is performed in a single call to the model, in zero-shot mode.
  In the evaluation step, we introduce a path-based semantic matching algorithm to align the nested, variable-cardinality attributes in the extracted results with those in the gold standard. We use generative AI for semantic comparison of the extracted and gold standard values of an attribute, and introduce a rubric to classify the result of the comparison, according to domain-specific considerations, as an exact, semantic, useful, or non-match.
  We were able to extract 12 out of 14 attributes with an F1 score of $>$90\% from documents published by the health technology assessment organisation NICE, using the generative AI model Claude Opus 3. The time needed to extract the attributes from a document was $\sim$30 times lower than the time taken by a human domain expert. We further demonstrate generalisability of this framework across different generative AI models and transferability across different HTA organisations and languages.

</details>


### [274] [FinEvo-Bench: A Longitudinal Benchmark for Self-Evolving Agents in Professional Financial Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06144)
*Bo Deng,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Chongyang Tao,Xuanren Chen,Chenggang Xie,Renzhao Liang,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinEvo-Bench 是一个面向金融领域的纵向基准测试，包含120个真实案例任务，覆盖6个金融领域、20个业务场景，用于评估智能体在连续任务中的自我进化能力。实验表明，进化条件下的智能体得分提升9.33-19.37分，合规问题减少0.12-0.44个/任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试大多独立评估任务，无法衡量跨任务经验的迁移效果；而现有的自我进化基准又未能同时覆盖专业工作流、开放式交付物和多维度评估。因此需要一个新的纵向基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建FinEvo-Bench，包含6个金融领域、20个业务场景、120个真实案例任务。每个场景包含6个相关但实质不同的案例，共享专业流程和人工审核的评分标准。使用Qwen3.7-Max作为 backbone，比较四种自进化智能体架构（Letta、Codex等），在三种独立打乱的全局交错任务流上进行测试。设置非进化对照组以估计自我进化增益，并使用基于Claude Opus 4.6的Claude Code评分代理进行评估。同时对比了技能进化、记忆进化、组合进化三种策略，以及评分反馈与参考答案反馈两种反馈方式。

Result: Letta取得最高进化分数（91.65）和最少合规问题（0.09个/任务）；Codex实现最大自我进化增益（+19.37）。进化条件下，所有架构得分提升9.33-19.37分，合规问题减少0.12-0.44个/任务。场景内第4-6个任务的得分增益比第1-3个任务高6.10-8.70分。技能单独进化的任务质量和合规性优于记忆进化和组合进化。评分反馈的效果优于参考答案反馈。

Conclusion: FinEvo-Bench有效衡量了智能体的专业表现与自我进化能力，揭示了不同架构和进化策略在利用先前经验提升后续表现方面的显著差异，为金融领域智能体的持续学习能力评估提供了标准化平台。

Abstract: Most agent benchmarks evaluate tasks independently and cannot measure whether experience from one task helps with later tasks. Existing self-evolution benchmarks do not jointly cover professional workflows, open-ended deliverables, and multi-aspect evaluation. We introduce FinEvo-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark with 120 real-case-grounded tasks, 20 business scenes across six financial domains. Institution-provided professional procedures define the required operations and constraints. Eligible institution-provided and publicly documented cases supply the task facts. Each scene contains six related but substantively distinct cases that share a professional procedure and a manually reviewed rubric for task quality and financial compliance. We compare four self-evolving agent scaffolds using the same Qwen3.7-Max backbone and three independently shuffled, globally interleaved task streams. Paired non-evolving controls estimate each scaffold's self-evolution gain from retained experience, while an independent Claude Code scoring agent backed by Claude Opus 4.6 evaluates all outputs. Letta achieves the highest evolved score (91.65) and fewest compliance issues (0.09 per task); Codex achieves the largest self-evolution gain (+19.37). Across scaffolds, the evolving condition raises scores by 9.33-19.37 points and reduces compliance issues by 0.12-0.44 per task. Paired score gains at within-scene ranks 4-6 exceed those at ranks 1-3 by 6.10-8.70 points. In Claude Code, skill-only evolution produces higher task quality and fewer compliance issues than memory-only and combined memory-skill evolution. Across all four scaffolds, rubric feedback also yields higher scores and fewer compliance issues than reference-answer feedback. FinEvo-Bench measures both professional performance and self-evolution ability: how effectively an agent turns prior experience into later improvement.

</details>


### [275] [HarnessOpt-Bench: Evaluating LLMs at Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06301)
*Varun Ursekar,Apaar Shanker,Yash Maurya,Shehab Yasser,Vijay S. Kalmath,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 HarnessOpt-Bench 基准，用于在昂贵且随机的评估条件下测量前沿 LLM 的端到端 harness 优化能力。实验表明优化器模型间的差异大于编码 harness 间的差异，原生 harness 并非始终占优，且不同任务和种子条件下增益差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 越来越多地部署在智能体系统中，系统能力不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于其周围的 harness（提示、工具、控制流、记忆和编排代码）。自动化 harness 优化是提升 AI 系统的重要途径，但社区缺乏统一的协议来衡量前沿 LLM 在这项任务上的表现。

Method: 构建 HarnessOpt-Bench 基准：优化器（LLM + 编码 harness）接收目标智能体的种子 harness、分级评估反馈和固定的目标评估预算，对 harness 进行编辑并提名最终候选方案。候选方案在不可见的留出测试集上计算相对于种子的归一化增益。可信执行环境强制实施评估边界，计量目标智能体的资源使用，并保留候选版本以供审计。在 4 个下游任务上评估 5 个前沿 LLM 作为优化器，分别在共享编码 harness 和各自原生 harness 下运行，共完成 111 次评分运行。

Result: 优化器模型之间的性能差异大于其所使用的编码 harness 之间的差异；原生 harness 并未表现出一致的优越性；不同任务和种子条件下的增益差异显著。这表明 harness 优化是一项可测量且具有区分度的能力，存在巨大的提升空间。

Conclusion: HarnessOpt-Bench 成功将 harness 优化确立为一项可量化、可区分的能力指标。当前前沿模型的 harness 优化表现远未饱和，存在广阔的改进空间，该基准为未来研究提供了统一的评估框架。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed within agentic systems, their capabilities depend not only on the model weights but also on the harness: the prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code surrounding them. This makes automated harness optimization -- the iterative and evaluation-guided improvement of a harness by an AI system -- both an important route to improving AI systems and a demanding capability for AI systems themselves. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measuring how well frontier LLMs perform at this task. We introduce HarnessOpt-Bench, a benchmark for end-to-end harness optimization under expensive and stochastic evaluation. An optimizer, an LLM paired with a coding harness, receives a target agent's seed harness, graded evaluation feedback, and a fixed target-evaluation budget. It edits the harness and nominates a final candidate, which is scored by its normalized gain over the seed on a held-out test partition that remains inaccessible throughout search. A trusted execution environment enforces the evaluation boundary, meters target-agent resource use, and preserves candidate versions for audit. We evaluate 5 frontier LLMs as optimizers both under a shared coding harness and under their native harnesses across 4 downstream tasks, over 111 scored runs. Experiment results show that optimizer models separate more than the coding harnesses they act through, native harnesses are not consistently superior, and gains vary substantially across tasks and seed regimes. These results establish harness optimization as a measurable and discriminative capability with large space for improvement.

</details>


### [276] [PaDoc: Layout-Grounded Parallel Decoding for Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06146)
*Hao Yu,Jiabo Zhan,Kang Liu,Linnan Zhao,Dongxu Yue,Rui Chen,Jinglin Wang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PaDoc 是一种基于布局的分支式文档解析器，将页面解析分解为布局流与区域内容分支的并行解码，在保持全页上下文的同时显著减少解码深度，实现了SOTA精度和吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端文档解析器将所有区域序列化为单一自回归序列，解码长度随内容线性增长，缺乏并行性；而两阶段裁剪方法虽有区域级并行，却带来重复视觉预填充和碎片化页面上下文。需要在保留全页上下文的前提下消除区域间的序列依赖。

Method: 提出 PaDoc，将预测的布局视为共享页面表示上的分支结构。核心包括：(1) 基于区域充分性假设，推导出布局流与区域内容分支并发推进的前缀条件因子分解；(2) 在单一 MLLM 内实现打包变长祖先注意力（packed variable-length ancestor attention），在标准 next-token 训练下保持可见性；(3) 采用掩码并行解码创建分支，由 vLLM 后端以并发请求形式服务，复用缓存中的共享前缀。

Result: 在 OmniDocBench Full 上，PaDoc 达到 91.1 的 Overall Layout F1，Overall Score 94.24（端到端解析器中 top-tier），Text Edit 0.038，Formula CDM 95.59。在 384 页子集及单卡 A800 上，在五个并发级别下均为最快的端到端解析器，相比同骨干的 Sequential SFT 基线，有效页面吞吐量提升 67.4-118%，P95 延迟降低 39.2-54.9%。

Conclusion: 通过将文档解析重构为布局条件的分支生成问题，PaDoc 实现了全页上下文与区域级并行性的统一，在精度、吞吐量和延迟方面均显著优于现有端到端及两阶段方法。

Abstract: End-to-end document parsers provide a unified interface, but serialize page layouts and regional contents into one autoregressive sequence. This formulation forces independent regions onto a decoding path whose length grows with the total content, whereas crop-based two-stage parsers expose region-level parallelism at the cost of repeated visual prefills and fragmented page context. To retain full-page context while removing dependencies, we propose PaDoc, a layout-grounded parser that treats the predicted layout as a branching structure over a shared page representation. Under a region-sufficiency assumption, we derive a prefix-conditioned factorization in which the layout stream and regional content branches advance concurrently, reducing the decoding depth to the longest layout-content path. We realize this factorization within a single MLLM: packed variable-length ancestor attention preserves the visibility under standard next-token training, while masked parallel decoding creates branches that the evaluated vLLM backend serves as concurrent requests with cache-resident shared-prefix reuse. On OmniDocBench Full, PaDoc attains an Overall layout F1 of 91.1 and, among end-to-end parsers, a top-tier Overall score of 94.24 together with the best Text Edit (0.038) and Formula CDM (95.59). On a 384-page subset and one A800 GPU, it is the fastest end-to-end parser at five concurrency levels, improving valid-page throughput by 67.4-118% and reducing P95 latency by 39.2-54.9% relative to a same-backbone Sequential SFT baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Longin-Yu/Padoc

</details>


### [277] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长文档（尤其是财务报表类）检索增强生成，传统分块-嵌入-最近邻检索存在结构性缺陷：表格数据中数值与其单位、财年等上下文因分块边界被割裂。作者提出READ框架，通过确定性操作（词汇搜索、结构导航、限定跨度读取）替代嵌入检索，在51个验证问题上准确率达58.8%，显著优于密集检索的15.7%。有趣的是，BM25与READ统计上无显著差异，说明增益来自"无嵌入"范式而非智能体迭代本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG流程（分块→嵌入→top-k检索）在处理财务报表、审计报告、监管申报等富含表格的长文档时存在结构性缺陷：(1) 86.8%的内容行是表格行；(2) 大量近似数字在嵌入空间中难以区分；(3) 数值与其单位/财年标题被分块边界切断——单位错误可导致数量级偏差。作者旨在量化并解决这一问题。

Method: 提出READ（Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search）框架，智能体通过三个确定性操作访问原始文档：① 标准化词汇搜索（normalized lexical search）；② 结构导航（structural navigation）；③ 限定跨度读取（bounded span reads）。这些操作通过模型上下文协议（MCP）暴露，使得每条检索轨迹可复现、可审计。同时构建了表格感知分块器（table-aware chunker）作为对照。

Result: 在51个验证问题上，READ达到58.8%准确率，显著优于密集检索的15.7%（p_Holm = 2×10⁻⁵）和调优后的35.3%（仍领先23.5个百分点，p_Holm = 0.017）。仅给予top-k工具而非MCP接口的智能体仅达27.5%，表明增益来自接口设计而非迭代。但BM25与READ统计上无显著差异，说明核心优势在于"无嵌入检索"范式，而非智能体与词汇搜索的对立。

Conclusion: 对于表格密集型长文档，嵌入无关的确定性检索框架READ显著优于基于嵌入的密集检索方法。关键设计在于通过MCP暴露结构化操作接口，而非依赖不透明的相似度评分。但READ与BM25无统计差异，表明论文的核心贡献是分离了"嵌入vs无嵌入"这一维度，而非"智能体vs词汇搜索"。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [278] [CogVis: Must Open-Vocabulary Change Detection Perceive the Scene Anew for Every Query?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06150)
*Zijie Wang,Chen Zhong,Wei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CogVis，一种受人类视觉变化感知启发的认知记忆引导框架，将开放词汇变化检测重新构建为感知-记忆-验证范式，通过场景变化感知器、语义记忆校准器和自适应区域滤波器实现解耦的时空推理，在七个基准上达到SOTA性能，并提升推理吞吐量28.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）方法将时序感知、语义判别和区域验证纠缠在一起，导致结果不稳定且计算冗余。受人类视觉变化感知机制的启发，作者希望将类别无关的时序变化证据与类别特定的语义决策解耦，实现更高效、更稳定的变化检测。

Method: 提出CogVis框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 场景变化感知器（SCP），从冻结的双时相特征中提取可复用的、类别无关的变化先验，将时序证据与语义类别决策解耦；(2) 语义记忆校准器（SMC），通过动态估计图像-查询特定的决策阈值来补偿类别依赖的分数偏移；(3) 自适应区域滤波器（ARF），利用学习到的语义、时序和结构可靠性对连通候选区域进行过滤。

Result: 在语义变化检测、二值变化定位和建筑损坏评估等七个基准数据集上，CogVis在所有评估数据集上均取得最先进（SOTA）性能。通过共享场景级变化感知，CogVis避免了跨查询重复的类别无关时序感知，推理吞吐量提升28.50%。

Conclusion: CogVis通过感知-记忆-验证范式有效解耦了OVCD中的时序感知与语义判别，在保持高精度的同时显著提升了推理效率，为开放词汇变化检测领域提供了新的高效架构思路。

Abstract: Earth-surface monitoring requires change detection models capable of recognizing arbitrary semantic categories. Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (OVCD) addresses this need. However, existing methods often entangle temporal perception, semantic discrimination, and region verification, causing unstable results and redundant computation. Inspired by human visual change perception, we propose CogVis, a cognitive memory-guided framework that reformulates OVCD as a perception-memory-verification paradigm. CogVis first employs a Scene Change Perceptron (SCP) to extract a reusable, category-agnostic change prior from frozen bi-temporal features, thereby decoupling temporal evidence from semantic category decisions. A Semantic Memory Calibrator (SMC) then compensates for category-dependent score shifts by dynamically estimating an image-query-specific decision threshold. Finally, an Adaptive Region Filter (ARF) filters connected candidates using learned semantic, temporal, and structural reliability. Experiments on seven benchmarks spanning semantic change detection, binary change localization, and building-damage assessment show that CogVis achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated datasets. By sharing scene-level change perception, CogVis further avoids repeating category-agnostic temporal perception across queries and improves inference throughput by 28.50%.

</details>


### [279] [iARCS: Iterative Agentic RL for Controllable 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06161)
*Saugat Adhikari,Ashok Prasad Neupane,Pramish Paudel,Ajad Chhatkuli,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iARCS 是一个迭代式智能体强化学习框架，通过对预训练场景生成器进行两阶段调优（通用奖励预训练 + 任务特定 LLM 奖励程序微调），使合成 3D 场景在满足自然语言任务约束的同时保持视觉真实性与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成 3D 场景生成器偏重感知真实感，却忽视了可达性、可穿越性、空间规则合规性等功能性约束，导致合成数据在下游具身 AI 训练中实用性不足。论文旨在弥合这一"视觉真实—功能合规"的鸿沟。

Method: 提出 iARCS 框架，分为两个阶段：(1) 通用奖励预训练（universal-reward pretraining），提升场景的物理合理性与布局质量；(2) 任务特定微调，使用 LLM 生成奖励程序（reward programs），并根据训练反馈迭代优化这些程序。整体采用智能体强化学习范式，将预训练场景生成器适配到自然语言描述的任务需求上。

Result: 实验表明，iARCS 在可步行性（walkability）、可到达性（reachability）和间隙合规（clearance）等任务上，约束保真度显著提升，任务特定约束优化有效，且场景多样性保持竞争力。此外，iARCS 生成的数据还能反哺提升基础生成器。

Conclusion: iARCS 不仅是一个可控场景编辑方法，更是一个实用的合成数据生成工具，可有效弥合生成质量与功能约束之间的差距，为具身 AI 训练提供更可靠的数据来源。

Abstract: Synthetic 3D scene generation is increasingly used as a data source for computer vision and embodied AI, but existing generators often optimize perceptual realism without reliably satisfying task-critical functional constraints. This mismatch limits the usefulness of synthetic data for downstream training, where accessibility, traversability, and spatial rule compliance are often essential. We present iARCS, an iterative agentic reinforcement learning framework that adapts a pretrained scene generator to natural-language task requirements. iARCS uses a two-stage strategy: universal-reward pretraining to improve physical plausibility and layout quality, followed by task-specific fine-tuning with LLM-generated reward programs that are iteratively refined from training feedback. Experiments show improved constraint fidelity on walkability, reachability, and clearance-focused tasks, effective task-specific constraint optimization, and competitive scene diversity. We further show that data generated by iARCS improves a base generator, supporting its value as a practical synthetic data generation tool rather than only a controllable scene editing method.

</details>


### [280] [MicroEvo: Knowledge-Guided LLM Sampling for Efficient Microarchitecture Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06183)
*Jia Xiong,Runkai Li,Chenxu Niu,Guangyuan Gao,Changwen Xing,Yifan Zhang,Xinlai Wan,Jieran Cui,Chen Bai,Yusheng Hua,Ying Wang,Ming Ling,Xi Wang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MicroEvo 将大语言模型与蒙特卡洛树搜索相结合，通过知识引导的进化搜索策略，显著提升了微架构多目标优化的 Pareto 前沿质量和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构设计空间探索面临搜索空间巨大、PPA 评估代价高昂的双重困境，现有方法（如 NSGA-II）采用盲目搜索，忽视微架构依赖关系，且无法有效从迭代搜索中学习，导致评估浪费和 Pareto 收敛性差。

Method: 提出 MicroEvo 框架，核心包含四大组件：(1) LLM 驱动的进化算子，利用大语言模型编码微架构设计知识；(2) Pareto 感知的树策略，在 MCTS 中平衡 Pareto 贡献与多样性；(3) 主动知识积累机制，提取并复用优化过程中获得的洞察；(4) 状态感知指令，在线自适应调整搜索行为。

Result: 相比 NSGA-II，MicroEvo 的 Pareto 前沿质量最高提升 36.2%，搜索效率提升 10.6 倍，并在复杂的工业级处理器核心上展现出强大的可扩展性。

Conclusion: MicroEvo 通过将 LLM 知识引导与 MCTS 智能搜索有机结合，有效解决了微架构设计空间探索中盲目搜索和知识复用不足的痛点，为大规模多目标微架构优化提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Microarchitecture design space exploration suffers from expansive search spaces and expensive PPA evaluation, leaving only a small simulation budget for design decision-making. Existing methods perform blind search without considering microarchitectural dependencies and fail to learn from the iterative search effectively, leading to wasted evaluations and weak Pareto convergence. In this paper, we propose MicroEvo, a knowledge-guided framework that couples off-the-shelf LLMs with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for multi-objective microarchitecture optimization. MicroEvo combines LLM-driven evolutionary operators, a Pareto-aware tree policy that balances Pareto contribution and diversity, an active knowledge accumulation mechanism that extracts and reuses optimization insights, and state-aware directives that adapt the search behavior online. Experiments show that MicroEvo improves Pareto-front quality by up to 36.2% over NSGA-II and achieves 10.6x higher search efficiency, and also demonstrates strong scalability to a complex industrial-scale core. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/GEAR-SEU/MicroEvo-ICCAD-26.

</details>


### [281] [Comparative Approaches to Agent Retrieval over Large Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06196)
*Indivara Kolluru,Nathan Sportsman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究了大规模技能库中技能检索与排序问题，对比了混合排序器与类型化知识图谱两种方案。结果表明，图谱未能扩展检索范围，因为其边几乎完全来自排序器已覆盖的嵌入邻域；同时揭示了作者自写查询会夸大命中率高达44个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模技能库（690个技能）驱动的智能体需要高效决定加载哪些技能及顺序，但全量加载成本高昂且缺乏自主排序的结构化支持。现有方案缺乏对检索与结构化依赖关系的系统性理解，需要明确在何种条件下添加结构能真正改进检索效果。

Method: 在690个技能的语料库上构建两个系统：（1）混合排序器，结合词汇检索与稠密嵌入检索，实现稀疏、按需的技能加载；（2）类型化知识图谱，编码前置条件、数据流、排序等工作流关系。在117个非回显真实查询上评估，设计对照实验：在图谱邻域替换排序器追加结果（相同token预算）条件下比较性能，并分析图谱边的来源与排序器召回的关系。

Result: 混合排序器Top-5命中率为73.5%±8.0%。图谱在等token预算下显著更差（-11.2个百分点，p=0.0007）；LLM生成的边层相对本地嵌入获得的邻居无增益；73%的排序器未命中查询在图谱中不可达；98.6%的类型化边连接的是排序器已共同检索到的技能。作者自写查询的hit@5被高估达44个百分点。

Conclusion: 添加结构化依赖（知识图谱）未能改进强排序器的检索效果，根本原因是图谱的候选边受限于与排序器相同的嵌入邻域拓扑（预过滤拓扑边界），只能丰富关系语义但无法扩展检索覆盖。当且仅当结构能引入排序器覆盖范围之外的新候选时，结构化互依赖才对检索有增益。

Abstract: Agents backed by large skill libraries must decide which skills to load and in what order. Loading the entire library into context is expensive and provides no structure for autonomous sequencing. We study two systems for this problem over a corpus of 690 skills: a hybrid ranker combining lexical and dense-embedding retrieval for sparse, on-demand loading, and a typed knowledge graph encoding workflow relations such as prerequisites, data flow, and ordering. On a set of 117 realistic, non-echoing queries, the hybrid ranker retrieves the correct skill within the top five in 73.5% +/- 8.0 of cases, leaving roughly a quarter of queries unserved. When used as the design intended (substituting graph neighbours for additional ranked results at matched token budget), the graph is significantly worse (-11.2 points, p = 0.0007). Its LLM-generated edge layer adds nothing over neighbours obtained free from a local embedding pass, and 73% of the queries the ranker misses are not reachable through the graph at all. We attribute this to a pre-filter topology bound. Because the graph's candidate edges are drawn from the same embedding neighbourhood the ranker already searches, 98.6% of typed edges connect skills the ranker had already surfaced together. The graph can enrich relation semantics but cannot extend retrieval reach. We further show that evaluating on author-written queries overstates hit@5 by up to 44 points, which would have hidden these results entirely. Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [282] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnvACE 提出了一种世界排练（world rehearsal）方法，让 LLM 智能体在训练时无需外部环境交互，而是通过策略网络自身交替扮演"行动者"和"环境"两个角色，端到端联合优化，从而内化动作-环境响应关系，形成可支持决策的智能体世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练长周期工具使用型 LLM 智能体严重依赖真实或合成的可执行环境进行交互，这些环境的构建和验证成本高昂，或依赖难以落地的外部模拟器。因此需要一种摆脱外部环境约束的替代训练范式。

Method: 提出 EnvACE（agentic reinforcement learning with world rehearsal）：策略在训练时交替执行两步——首先生成工具调用（行动），然后扮演环境角色产生该行动所引发的响应，后续决策以排练产生的响应为条件。两个角色通过任务成功奖励进行端到端联合优化。通过世界排练，策略将动作与其环境响应之间的关系内化到参数中，形成智能体世界模型。

Result: 在 BFCL-v4、tau²-Bench、VitaBench 和 FinMCP-Bench 四个基准上，EnvACE 取得了强且可迁移的性能，在总体评估中优于环境扩展基线。受控实验表明，世界排练在不同模型规模上均一致改善策略学习。测试时，内化的世界模型支持在正式执行前进行私有排练，在适度排练预算下实现进一步收益，且无需额外外部交互。

Conclusion: 世界排练为扩展 LLM 智能体训练提供了一条超越外部环境约束的新路径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [283] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-RAG，一种将检索增强生成（RAG）引入时间序列预测的新方法。通过设计专用的参考令牌（reference tokens），有效融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列信息，在多个真实预测基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在大型语言模型中已被证明能有效整合外部信息以提升能力，但在时间序列预测领域应用有限。现有时间序列模型受限于训练数据少、参数量小、生成能力弱，直接将检索序列拼接到提示中的做法（如语言模型那样）效果不佳，因此需要一种适合时间序列特点的RAG融合机制。

Method: 提出TS-RAG框架，核心创新是引入专门设计的参考令牌（reference tokens），用于将输入序列与检索到的相似历史序列进行信息融合，而非简单拼接，从而更有效地捕获复杂的时间动态模式。

Result: TS-RAG在多个真实世界时间序列预测基准上，一致地达到了当时最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: 将RAG思想适配到时间序列预测领域是可行且有价值的，关键在于设计适合时序数据特点的信息融合方式（参考令牌机制），而非照搬语言模型中的做法。TS-RAG验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [284] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DASH提出了一种基于散度自适应监督范围的方法，通过将局部蒸馏信号与序列均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门控，控制反向多步聚合，从而根据局部散度的时序演变动态调整token级监督权重，在多个数学推理基准上优于标准OPSD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）虽然通过密集的token级分布监督缓解了RLVR中奖励信号的稀疏性问题，但它对所有局部散度分配相同的系数，忽视了rollout的时序结构——相同的散度幅度在不同位置、不同散度历史下代表不同意义，无法自适应地根据已实现的差异序列调整权重。

Method: 提出DASH（Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons）方法：将每个局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门控（adaptive propagation gate），利用这些门控控制反向多步聚合过程，使token级监督权重能够根据局部散度在生成过程中的演变情况进行自适应调整。该方法复用OPSD已计算的师生分布，无需额外的模型前向传播。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准上，跨越三种模型规模的实验表明，DASH在所有基准和所有规模上均优于重新实现的匹配版标准OPSD基线。

Conclusion: 通过引入时序感知的自适应监督权重，DASH有效利用了rollout中散度演变的时序信息，在不增加计算开销的前提下提升了OPSD的性能，为RLVR中的密集监督提供了更优的token级信号分配策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [285] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何在保持现有效用检查通过的前提下，提升合成临床基准的结构真实性。作者将基准修订形式化为"效用约束下的真实性改进"问题，通过对Synthea生成的医疗数据进行缺失结构、可操作性、结构合理性和人群对齐等多维度评估，发现基线基准极度稀疏。两种确定性修订策略在维持效用底线的前提下改善了这些指标，表明合成基准质量应显式优化，效用仅作为约束而非真实性的充分证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成临床基准即使通过效用检查，其结构仍可能严重偏离真实临床数据，尤其是在隐私敏感的医疗环境中难以获取运营数据进行验证。需要一种在不破坏下游效用检查的前提下提升基准结构真实性的方法。

Method: 将基准修订形式化为"效用约束下的真实性改进"问题：在保持高于运营效用底线的前提下，最大化数据集真实性。在Synthea生成的护理差距基准上实例化该方法，通过四个维度衡量真实性：缺失结构（missingness structure）、简洁性（simplicity）、结构合理性（structural plausibility）和人群对齐（population alignment）。对比两种确定性修订策略与简单稠密化控制的差异，并区分内部基准真实性与对聚合运营参考的源保真度这两个相关但不同的目标。

Result: 基线基准极度稀疏：样本对缺失率79.44%，仅12.75%的行可操作，38.94%的患者无可操作指标，前三token集中度达100.0%。两种确定性修订策略在维持当前效用底线的前提下改善了这些面板指标，而简单稠密化控制则保留了不真实的模板化结构。内部基准真实性与源保真度被证明是相关但不同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准的质量应被显式优化，效用应仅作为一个约束条件而非真实性的充分证据。基准修订可以在不牺牲既有效用检查通过率的前提下，系统性地提升结构真实性。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [286] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果图框架审计多模态LLM的视觉工具使用行为，发现尽管聚合准确率有所提升，但视觉工具使用在大多数推理过程中并不具备因果有效性，揭示了"伪视觉工具使用"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前配备主动视觉操作（如裁剪缩放）的多模态LLM，其性能增益往往微弱甚至为负，且存在重复裁剪无关区域、回答不如直接推理等问题。作者质疑返回的视觉证据是否真正因果性地影响模型回答，因此提出通过因果审计来系统诊断这一问题。

Method: 将视觉工具使用形式化为因果图，分离观察中介路径与动作诱导捷径。设计三层干预审计框架：(1) 策略层——比较工具使用与直接推理；(2) 轨迹层——在推理过程中破坏所有观察；(3) 步骤层——在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观察，提出"视觉证据增益"（Visual Evidence Gain）估计量以隔离每次返回观察的因果贡献。

Result: 在6个代表性模型和5个细粒度感知基准上，揭示策略误校准的两种失败模式：(1) "只看不调用"（Calling Without Looking）——返回的观察对答案无因果影响；(2) "调用无规划"（Looking Without Planning）——观察有信息量但调用时序不协调。轨迹层诊断表明准确率增益集中在少数"校准良好"的样本上，形成"视觉工具使用的幻觉"。

Conclusion: 尽管视觉工具使用在聚合层面看似提升准确率，但其因果有效性在广泛推理轨迹上并不成立。该现象被定义为"视觉工具使用的幻觉"，提示现有模型的主动视觉操作策略存在根本性的误校准问题。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [287] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuanTiMedAI框架，将智能体LLM用于临床特征发现，结合紧凑的量子循环网络进行心脏骤停死亡率预测，在MIMIC-IV队列上以仅605参数取得0.852 AUROC，超越现有基线约2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU心脏骤停死亡率预测研究多依赖入院早期的静态摘要数据，忽略了患者住院期间生理状态随时间演变的动态过程（恶化与恢复的时序模式），导致预测能力受限。

Method: 提出QuanTiMedAI框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 智能体LLM（Agentic LLM），用于临床信息驱动的特征发现与选择；(2) 紧凑型量子循环网络（Quantum Recurrent Network），利用非线性特征增强实现时序感知的死亡率预测。通过结构化消融实验验证各架构设计选择的贡献。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者队列上，QuanTiMedAI仅使用605个参数即达到0.852 AUROC，较当前最先进基线提升约2.9%。智能体LLM引导的特征选择持续优于传统方法，量子架构以极低参数量实现了有竞争力的预测性能。

Conclusion: 量子增强的序列建模能够在参数量大幅减少的情况下超越经典循环网络，验证了将智能体AI与量子机器学习相结合用于临床时序预测的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [288] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将概念激活向量（CAV）的偏差检测方法扩展到两类神经口语评估系统（BERT文本评分器和Whisper多模态评分器），并首次引入稀疏自编码器（SAE）在稀疏潜在空间中学习CAV以改善概念方向的可恢复性。研究发现，概念可恢复性和概念敏感性与模型架构及表示层密切相关，且SAE虽能提升线性可分离性，但会衰减原始激活空间的敏感性，揭示了概念可恢复性与概念影响力之间的关键区别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统在高风险考试中广泛应用，但其公平性存疑——评分可能受考生母语（L1）或年龄等无关属性影响。基于Transformer的基础模型虽提升了评分精度，但其黑箱特性使得公平性与可解释性分析更加困难。此前基于特征评分器的CAV偏差检测方法尚未在复杂神经网络评分系统上系统验证，且CAV依赖线性可分性假设，在深层嵌入空间中可能不成立。

Method: 1. 将CAV分析扩展至BERT（纯文本）和Whisper（语音+文本多模态）两种神经口语评分系统；2. 区分两个维度：概念编码（线性可恢复性）与概念影响（基于梯度的敏感度度量）；3. 引入稀疏自编码器（SAE），在稀疏潜在空间中学习CAV后映射回激活空间，以获取更干净的概念方向；4. 在不同表示层和架构上进行系统性对比实验。

Result: 1. 概念可恢复性高度依赖于被探测的表示层和架构类型，而非仅取决于概念本身；2. 概念敏感度同样呈现架构依赖性；3. SAE使概念在潜在空间中更具线性可恢复性，但会衰减原始激活空间中的敏感度，尤其在低维层中表现明显；4. 概念可恢复性与概念影响力并非同构，需区分对待。

Conclusion: 在审计口语评估系统的偏差时，必须严格区分“概念是否被编码”（可恢复性）与“概念是否影响评分”（影响力）两个维度。SAE有助于发现更纯净的概念方向，但不能简单替代原始激活空间的分析。研究为构建更公平、可解释的L2口语自动评分系统提供了方法论指导和实证依据。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [289] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TrajDebug是一种面向LLM Agent系统的错误生命周期追踪框架，通过多粒度历史压缩和证据驱动的错误识别解决长轨迹中的关键错误定位问题，并构建了包含486条人工标注失败轨迹的TrajErrBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent系统在复杂任务中容易产生级联错误且难以调试。关键错误检测旨在定位失败轨迹中导致最终失败的最早错误步骤，但面临两大挑战：(1) 长轨迹中判断某步骤的证据分散在远距离的指令、观察和上下文中；(2) 失败轨迹通常包含多个具有不同下游影响的局部错误，仅部分错误最终导致失败。

Method: 提出TrajDebug框架，核心包含两方面：(1) 多粒度历史压缩技术，将长轨迹中的分散证据进行压缩聚合；(2) 基于证据的错误识别与归因方法，通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和终端影响来实现关键错误归因。此外，构建了TrajErrBench基准数据集。

Result: 在多个Agent基准上的实验表明，TrajDebug在关键错误检测任务上取得最优综合性能，其诊断结果能为提升下游Agent成功率提供可操作的反馈。

Conclusion: TrajDebug通过错误生命周期追踪有效解决了长轨迹Agent系统中的关键错误定位问题，其多粒度压缩和证据驱动方法优于现有基线，并展示了实际应用价值。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [290] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了可解释人工智能（XAI）在评估方面的不足，以DetoxAI图像识别系统为案例展示偏差检测与概念遗忘，并提出了面向概念漂移环境下解释方法的自适应策略，特别讨论了反事实解释的适应性经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI领域存在评估不充分的问题，且缺乏针对数据流演变（概念漂移）场景下解释方法的适应性研究。本文旨在通过这些问题的揭示和探索，推动XAI评估的规范化以及动态环境下解释方法的改进。

Method: 首先以DetoxAI图像识别系统为例，说明偏差检测和概念遗忘过程中XAI评估不足的具体表现；然后展示一个基于人类评估（human-grounded evaluation）的图像分类解释方法评估案例；接着探讨在概念漂移的数据流中如何适应性地调整解释方法，重点讨论反事实解释（counterfactuals）在动态场景下的应用经验；最后将上述问题关联到数据、模型与解释三者协同演化的跟踪挑战。

Result: 揭示了XAI评估不足的典型问题；提供了基于人类评估的解释方法验证实例；总结了反事实解释在概念漂移环境中应用的实践经验；提出了数据-模型-解释协同演化跟踪的研究挑战。

Conclusion: XAI领域亟需更严格、更系统的评估方法，尤其是在动态数据环境下。解释方法不仅要适配静态模型，更需要跟踪数据、模型和解释三者的协同演化，反事实解释在该方向具有潜力但仍面临挑战。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [291] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件轨迹的受控视频评测方法，通过弹跳球碰撞、视觉闪烁和状态转换三类任务（2190个视频）系统诊断视频模型的事件计数能力。研究发现模型存在阶段性时间故障：对持续性状态转换可达12事件可靠计数，但对瞬时闪烁事件几乎无可靠计数能力；高频高计数场景下仅0.2%的最终答案正确，且提高采样率虽能提升表面得分但无法带来忠实的事件恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有真实世界视频基准虽然覆盖面广，但其固定片段混杂了事件数量、速率、持续时间和视觉复杂度等因素，导致难以隔离具体失败模式。程序化基准虽提供更好控制，但仅评分最终答案而非对照可执行的真值轨迹审计模型报告的事件序列，缺乏细粒度诊断能力。

Method: 提出trace-grounded parametric profiling方法：构建三类受控视频任务（弹跳球壁面碰撞、视觉闪烁、类别状态转换），在保持渲染条件不变的前提下系统变化事件数量N和频率F两个参数，共生成2190个视频。每个视频配备可执行的事件轨迹真值，支持能力曲面估计和时间戳级别的逐事件评估，而非仅对最终计数答案评分。

Result: （1）Gemini 3.6 Flash在80%可靠性阈值下，对持续性状态转换可在0.5-1.0Hz下达12事件可靠计数，但对瞬时闪烁事件无可靠正计数区域；（2）高频高计数场景下仅0.2%最终计数正确，模型仅恢复18.1%真实事件；（3）提高采样率使弹跳球准确率从19.6%升至29.3%，但序列与真值一致率仅3.7%；（4）不同提示策略收益同样有限，真实视频评测呈现相同的低事件计数成功集中现象。

Conclusion: 事件表征类型（持续vs瞬时）决定模型是否能初步获取视觉证据，这一限制随计数和频率增加而加剧。单纯提高帧采样率可虚增最终得分但无法实现忠实的逐事件恢复。trace-grounded profiling将视频评估从聚合准确率指标转向对时间推理失败点的精细化诊断。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [292] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: nMAS 是一个基于证据链接和评分准则的多智能体系统，能自动从 EHR 数据中工程化心力衰竭相关特征，在 HFrEF 和 HFpEF 表型分类中显著提升 AUROC，并具备可审计性与合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR 特征工程占数据科学家 39-45% 的工作量，是临床研究和 AI 的主要瓶颈。心力衰竭影响约 670 万美国成年人，需整合碎片化 EHR 数据与基于指南的临床推理，现有规则和 LLM 方法仅能部分自动化，且缺乏可维护性与证据可追溯性。

Method: 开发 nMAS（Nimblemind 多智能体系统），一个证据链接、评分准则驱动的自动化心力衰竭特征工程流水线。在 9 张 EHR 源表的 500 条虚拟患者记录上进行评估，生成 132 个结构化特征和 70 个经准则评分的聚合特征，并通过受限 LLM 进行结构完整性、准则合规性和来源审计。

Result: 加入聚合特征后，HFrEF 表型分类的保留集 AUROC 从 0.895 提升至 0.963，HFpEF 从 0.870 提升至 0.910。独立 LLM 准则评估在证据支持和方法学合理性上得分达最高分的 81.5%。

Conclusion: nMAS 证明了针对复杂心血管 EHR 数据进行自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性，但评估仅限于单机构队列，仍需外部验证。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-MLB 是一个可复现的开放基准测试框架，基于 GSE17048 全血 RNA 表达数据，用于评估机器学习模型在多发性硬化症（MS）与健康对照分类任务上的性能。梯度提升模型在留出集上表现最佳（MS Research Score 93.83，AUC-ROC 0.989）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管血液 RNA 表达数据可能包含与疾病相关的免疫信号，但不能替代临床诊断。现有研究虽已尝试用机器学习分析 MS 血液转录组数据，但缺乏一个统一、可复现、防泄漏的开放基准框架来进行公平的算法比较。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个标准化的研究对比平台。

Method: 构建 MS-MLB 基准框架，使用公开数据集 GSE17048 构建 MS vs 健康对照的二分类任务。采用统一的防泄漏评估管线，包括：嵌套交叉验证、未触碰的分层留出集（holdout set）、Bootstrap 置信区间、ROC 与精确率-召回率分析、校准度测量（Brier score），以及探索性的 MS Research Score。框架支持外部模型提交路径。

Result: 梯度提升（Gradient Boosting）在留出集上 MS Research Score 排名第一，达到 93.83，其中 AUC-ROC 为 0.989、敏感度 0.950、特异度 0.778、F1 分数 0.927、Brier 分数 0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB 是首个针对 GSE17048 全血 RNA 表达数据进行 MS vs 健康对照分类的开放基准，内置了文档化的外部模型提交机制。该评分仅供研究比较之用，未经临床验证。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [294] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFTER是一种后置修正器，通过挖掘冻结预测器残差中的可解释特征来驱动轻量级修正，结合组合搜索与大语言模型双生成器，在多种骨干网络上显著降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结预训练预测器常以结构化、重复的方式失败，仅靠微调修复代价高昂。现有自动化特征工程建模数据生成过程，忽视了模型失败过程本身。因此需要一种直接挖掘模型残差中的可解释特征、以轻量后置修正替代昂贵微调的方法。

Method: 提出CRAFTER框架：保持骨干网络冻结，使用两个互补生成器挖掘残差特征——(1)对原始输入通道的组合搜索；(2)用大语言模型生成命名特征组合、二值标志和短可执行代码。所有候选特征由统一验证门控机制接受或拒绝，验证选择的修正器利用被接受的特征进行预测修正或保持原预测不变。该源无关管道也允许先前特征工程系统在相同条件下评估。

Result: 在6个公开数据集和6个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在所有特征预算下均超越各类专用特征工程系统，修正收益约为单独修正器的两倍，最弱骨干网络误差降低高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下表现稳健，且即使叠加在已微调骨干上依然有效。

Conclusion: CRAFTER表明，直接对模型失败过程建模（而非数据生成过程）是一种高效的后置修正策略，源无关管道设计也为公平比较不同特征源提供了工具性框架。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [295] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程LLM工作流的概率语言，使开发者能够在不增加额外代码的情况下，量化和传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并实验不同的推理扩展技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的输出缺乏准确性且无法提供置信度度量，在多次LLM调用和其他工具组合的流程中，这种不确定性会不断累积，导致开发者和终端用户难以信任其结果。现有方法无法系统地在LLM工作流中量化和传播不确定性。

Method: 设计一种概率编程语言，专用于构建基于LLM的应用流程。该语言支持在流程逻辑中内嵌不确定性量化与传播机制，并允许开发者在不修改流程代码的前提下，无缝切换和实验不同的推理扩展（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 通过实验研究验证了该方法的能力，并构建了一个面向Rocq定理证明器的定理证明智能体作为案例研究。

Conclusion: 该概率语言有效提升了LLM工作流的可靠性和可信度，使开发者能够以声明式的方式管理不确定性，降低了构建可信LLM应用的门槛。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [296] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CGTime 是一个 4B 参数的计算-语言双通道时间序列模型，通过将感知（统计计算）与描述（LLM 生成）解耦，解决了现有方法在可靠性、真实性和可扩展性之间的三难困境，在多变量时间序列理解任务上显著优于更大规模的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练时间序列-语言对齐模型存在"自监督陷阱"：LLM 标注能力的上限限制了数据质量，模型永远无法学到标注者未知的知识。此外，现有数据集多为单变量，而真正重要的跨通道相关、超前滞后、共现异常等模式只在多变量场景中显现，进一步暴露了标注 LLM 的局限性。这导致现有方法在可靠性、真实性和可扩展性三者之间无法兼得（三难困境）。

Method: 提出感知与描述解耦的范式：用确定性代码从真实的开源多变量时间序列中计算一组统计量（如相关性、滞后结构等），将这些预先计算好的事实交给 LLM 进行语言化表达。感知任务由确定性计算完成（LLM 不擅长的部分），语言表达由 LLM 完成（LLM 擅长的部分）。基于此构建了 CGTime，一个 4B 参数的计算-时间序列-语言模型。

Result: CGTime 在多变量理解任务上大幅优于更大型通用模型。在留出基准测试上，多变量事实得分达到 0.283，显著高于 GPT-4o-mini（0.173）和 GPT-5.4-nano（0.203），且经 Holm 校正配对显著性检验后差距依然显著。此外，CGTime 在生成描述中陈述可验证数值事实的准确度更高，覆盖的统计属性范围也更广。

Conclusion: 将感知（计算）与表达（语言）解耦是解决时间序列-语言对齐自监督陷阱的有效策略。CGTime 证明，一个仅 4B 参数的模型通过计算增强可在多变量时间序列理解上超越更大模型，实现了可靠性、真实性和可扩展性的三者兼得。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [297] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR)框架，将3D感知与推理显式解耦：利用现成的感知模型重建结构化3D证据，再通过LoRA微调LLM在该几何证据上进行推理。该范式在空间推理基准上取得竞争性表现，同时提升了可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过大规模训练联合获取隐式的3D感知与推理能力，但作者观察到：现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，而LLM擅长组合性与符号化推理。两者的互补优势尚未被充分解耦利用，因此探索将感知与推理显式分离的替代范式。

Method: 提出DiSR框架：(1) 使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据（显式几何表示）；(2) 通过LoRA微调LLM，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据执行空间推理，无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具调用策略。

Result: DiSR在主流空间推理基准上取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略，展现出强性能、可解释性、模块化和计算效率的综合优势。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是面向空间智能的一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，相较端到端建模具有更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率，为空间推理研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [298] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 因子化生成模型仅通过匹配潜在风格变量 z_s 的边缘分布到高斯先验来声称风格与类别独立，但这种解释是错误的。作者证明边缘匹配不约束类别条件分布，风格变量仍可高度预测标签。通过精确分解和实证分析，揭示了这一问题，并提出了缓解策略和后验条件先验等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示因子化生成模型中一个普遍存在的误解：将潜在风格变量的边缘分布匹配到高斯先验，并不足以保证风格与类别信息的独立性。这种错误认知可能导致模型在实际分解任务中失效。

Method: 作者推导了因子化采样需满足的四个条件的精确分解，证明边缘分布匹配仅是其中之一。通过案例研究和四个代表性基线模型进行实证分析，使用全局MMD、线性探针分类准确率、聚类准确率和外部评估的类别条件生成等指标。在模型容量、课程学习、先验几何和超vision等六种独立扰动下验证泄露现象，并测试四种缓解策略及后验条件先验、经验风格库等方法。

Result: 模型在实现近乎零全局MMD的同时，线性探针仍能以74%-100%准确率恢复类别标签（随机水平10%）。模型聚类准确率达99.15%，但外部评估的类别条件生成成功率仅16%。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。后验条件先验在MNIST上实现0.97的外部类别生成得分，但在CIFAR-10上仅0.41，而经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜在变量边缘分布的散度度量无法证明其与类别标签的独立性；仅报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的分解性质。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [299] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowAdam 是一种混合优化器，在 Adam 遇到病态曲率时将自适应矩估计与常微分方程梯度流积分相结合，其核心创新"软动量注入"可在模式切换时平滑融合 ODE 速度与 Adam 动量，避免训练崩溃。在耦合优化任务中，该方法显著降低了泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam 类自适应矩方法的对角预条件器依赖于坐标系，当面对稠密或旋转的参数耦合（如矩阵分解、张量分解、图神经网络）时，因其独立处理每个参数而表现不佳。纯 ODE 方法虽能处理此类耦合，但难以推广到大规模、高随机性场景。因此需要一种能兼顾两者优势的混合方案。

Method: 提出 FlowAdam，在 Adam 框架中引入基于 ODE 的连续梯度流积分机制。当 EMA 统计量检测到损失景观困难时，切换至带裁剪的 ODE 积分。核心贡献是"软动量注入"（Soft Momentum Injection）：在模式切换时，将 ODE 速度与 Adam 动量进行混合，而非硬性替换，从而防止训练崩溃。

Result: 在耦合优化基准上，ODE 积分提供隐式正则化：低秩矩阵/张量恢复的保留误差降低 10-22%，Jester 协同过滤任务降低 6%，超越调优后的 Lion 和 AdaBelief；在 CIFAR-10 等良性条件任务上与 Adam 持平。MovieLens-100K 实验证实收益来自耦合参数交互而非偏差估计。消融实验表明，硬替换会使准确率从 100% 降至 82.5%。

Conclusion: FlowAdam 通过在 Adam 中引入 ODE 梯度流积分和软动量注入，有效解决了自适应优化器在耦合参数场景下的坐标系依赖问题，显著提升泛化性能，同时保持了在良性条件任务上的已有优势。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [300] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态指令中多需求优先级不等的问题，本文提出 PRISM 框架，将模型从"生成者"重新定位为"执行者"，通过四阶段数据合成生成带优先级的规则验证数据，并引入无需裁判模型的 PRISM-Eval 评估基准。仅用10K合成样本，PRISM 将 Qwen3-VL-4B 的 Strict 准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且效果可迁移至多种开源多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界多模态指令常包含多个重要性不等的需求，但现有训练数据将指令遵循简化为回答单一自包含问题。这种"生成者"范式使模型缺乏对多规则优先级的结构化理解与验证能力。

Method: 提出 PRISM 四阶段数据合成框架：(1) 生成 persona-task 配对；(2) 前缀引导的规则集生成；(3) 质量过滤的评分标准(rubrics)；(4) 结构化验证轨迹生成。模型被重新定位为"执行者"——给定图像和带类型、优先级的评分标准，逐条验证规则后产出整体判断。PRISM-Eval 采用 Loose 和 Strict 两种指标，通过对固定标签的确定性匹配实现评估，无需推理时的裁判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM 将 Qwen3-VL-4B 在 PRISM-Eval 上的 Strict 准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能。效果可迁移至4个额外的开源多模态大模型（覆盖 dense 和 MoE 架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化评分标准监督是实现多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径，PRISM 框架通过小规模高质量合成数据即可显著提升模型的规则验证与优先级感知能力。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [301] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了不同优化器在因子化模型上的隐式偏差差异：梯度下降隐式偏向低秩解，而Adam不偏向低秩，根源在于优化器是否具有规范等变性（gauge equivariance）。作者给出了无记忆等变规则的结构定理，并在矩阵感知、Transformer注意力头不变性、高光谱数据集上进行了系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化模型上的梯度下降存在隐式低秩偏向，但Adam从相同的小初始化出发却不具备该偏向。作者旨在系统解释这一差异的根源，揭示优化器的规范等变性（gauge equivariance）是低秩恢复的关键结构条件，并阐明基选择（basis choice）对插值解选择的深远影响。

Method: 作者利用因子化参数化中的规范对称性（gauge symmetry）：损失在 (U,V) → (UQ, VQ) 下不变。通过分析优化器更新规则是否满足规范等变性，将梯度下降、动量、shared-scalar Adam、Muon、Shampoo归为等变类，Adam、RMSProp等逐坐标方法归为非等变类。提出结构定理：无记忆等变规则恰好是Gram矩阵决定的左预条件器。建立传递定理，将梯度流的路径性质传递到common-scalar流。在欠定矩阵感知任务中，通过一参数族从逐坐标到共享标量预条件进行插值，分离各向异性的影响。提出"谱调度"调和关于Muon的矛盾报告。在Transformer中追踪注意力头不变量的演化，并在高光谱数据集上对比优化器的外推性能。

Result: （1）规范等变性是低秩恢复的必要条件而非充分条件；（2）一参数族实验单调恢复低秩偏向，确认各向异性是Adam失偏的原因；（3）谱调度显示：等速率更新可精确恢复低秩目标，但随谱尾部增大优势减弱；（4）在Transformer中，Adam第一步即分离两个规范等价初始化，最终W_Q^T W_K的相对Frobenius距离达56%，等变优化器则保持在浮点精度；（5）在高光谱数据集上，匹配训练损失下梯度下降在最低采样密度时降低留出误差43-44%，且有效秩更低。

Conclusion: 基选择并非调参细节，而是决定优化器选择何种插值解的关键决策。规范等变性提供了一种统一视角来理解和预测不同优化器在因子化模型上的隐式偏向，对深度学习中优化器的选择具有直接指导意义。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [302] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT 提出了一种参数高效的多任务监督微调框架，通过将过拟合感知调度器的状态压缩为可合并的适配器状态（而非完整模型检查点），在冻结预训练主干网络的前提下实现任务级回滚与继续训练，最终在五个主干网络上均取得优于原始 msft 的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务 SFT 将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，忽略了不同任务达到最佳泛化的时间点不同。原始 msft 通过任务级推出、排除和回滚解决了这一不匹配，但需要保存完整模型检查点，导致阶段切换的存储、恢复和部署成本高昂。本文旨在将这一过程参数高效化。

Method: 提出 AuroSFT 框架：(1) 冻结预训练主干，仅训练注入的适配器；(2) 在各任务验证性能达到峰值时回滚适配器检查点，并将该任务从当前混合中排除；(3) 继续在剩余活跃任务上训练；(4) 层级别上，每个适配器对低秩权重因子（而非样本表示）应用 AuroRA 风格的自适应非线性层，使更新保持对输入的线性、秩有界，且可精确合并回冻结投影矩阵。

Result: 在保留主干网络的对比协议下，AuroSFT 平均准确率达到 61.36%，优于对应 msft 参考行的 59.85%，且在全部五个主干网络上均取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT 成功将过拟合感知的多任务调度与参数高效微调相结合，以紧凑、可合并的适配器状态替代完整模型检查点，在显著降低存储和部署成本的同时实现了更优的多任务泛化性能。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [303] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroOFT 在量化正交微调（QOFT）基础上引入零初始化门控低秩非线性残差分支，在保持量化兼容性和初始化等价性的同时，赋予模型输入依赖的非线性校正能力，在 Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B 上显著优于 QOFT 和 QLoRA，且可训练参数量更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 QOFT 方法虽能实现低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其更新仅限于线性正交变换，缺乏对输入依赖的非线性校正能力，限制了模型的表达能力。本文旨在突破这一局限。

Method: 提出 AuroOFT，保留 QOFT 作为量化兼容的稳定分支，同时为每个适配的线性层附加一个零初始化门控低秩非线性残差分支。具体做法包括：(1) 将激活映射到 RMS 归一化的紧凑潜在空间；(2) 使用自适应非线性基函数，配合有界或 token 依赖的门控机制；(3) 零初始化上投影矩阵，使初始化时功能完全等价于 QOFT，确保训练稳定性。正交性在分支层面保持，不要求组合后的非线性层整体正交。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5 1.5B 和 3B 设置下，使用匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议，AuroOFT 的 Macro-6 指标较匹配的 QOFT 提升 1.30–2.70%，较 QLoRA 提升 6.52–10.62%，且可训练参数比 QLoRA 节省 32.3–44.7%。小规模选择题数学集仅作为协议敏感性诊断工具使用。

Conclusion: AuroOFT 成功将 QOFT 的量化兼容优势与非线性残差学习的表达能力相结合，在保持训练稳定性和参数效率的同时，取得了显著优于 QOFT 和 QLoRA 的性能，证明了该混合架构在低比特语言模型适配中的有效性。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>
